Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ACILIS
Hangi Tablo için : STOK2A
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Stok Açýlýþlarýný Excel 'den Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set gggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\acilis.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	colStokKodu = 5
	colLotNo = 11
	colSeriNo = 1
	colMiktar = 8
	colAmbalajNo = 4

	STOK2AT.Empty
	For i = 2 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'den okunuyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,colStokKodu).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit For

		StokKodu1 = GetFrStokKodu(StokKodu)
		if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
			satirNo = STOK2AT.AddRow
			STOK2AT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			STOK2AT.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu1
			STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,colMiktar).Value)
			AmbalajTuru = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colAmbalajNo).Value)
			SeriNo = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colSeriNo).Value)
			LotNo = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colLotNo).Value)
			STOK2AT.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", satirNo, SeriNo
			if trim(AmbalajTuru) = "0000" and STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) <= 200 Then
				AmbalajTuru = ""
				NVaryant1 = Doc.Cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,colMiktar).Value)
				SeriNo = ""
				LotNo = ""
			Else
				NVaryant1 = ""
			End if
			STOK2AT.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = AmbalajTuru
			STOK2AT.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = NVaryant1
			STOK2AT.SERINO(satirNo) = SeriNo
			STOK2AT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = LotNo
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr, Stok Kodu : " & trim(StokKodu) & " üretici kodu bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GetFrStokKodu(StokKodu)
	GetFrStokKodu = ""
	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vdfssj", "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'")
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFrStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : REZERVE3
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Seçilen Makarayý Sipariþe Rezerve Et

Sub Makro1()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK20T) Then Set STOK20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	if trim(STOK20T.KOD) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Miktar = GetGuncelMakaraMiktari()
	if Miktar = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu makara daha önce rezerve edilmiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STOK20T(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT2.Empty

	MTableT2.AddRow
	MTableT2.CopyRowFromOtherTable 1,STOK20T, STOK20T.GetCurrentRow
	MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(1) = Miktar
	MTableT2.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1) = Miktar
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	MPSSipArtNo= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "MUSTERISIPIARTNO", STOK20T.MPSNO & STOK20T.MPSTM_TRNUM, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")

'	Set rrr = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	QStr = "SELECT T.EVRAKNO, T.ARTNO, T.KOD, S.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, T.MUSTERIKODU, C.AD MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD,"
	QStr = QStr & " T.SF_MIKTAR, T.SF_IMIKTAR, T.SF_SF_UNIT, (T.SF_MIKTAR - T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK) SF_BAKIYE,"
	QStr = QStr & " (T.SF_MIKTAR - T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - COALESCE(S01.SF_MIKTAR,0)) ACIK_MIKTAR, "
	QStr = QStr & " CASE WHEN T.ARTNO='" & MPSSipArtNo & "' THEN" 
	QStr = QStr & " 		CASE WHEN (T.SF_MIKTAR - T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - COALESCE(S01.SF_MIKTAR,0))>" & MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(1) & " THEN"
	QStr = QStr & " 			" & MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(1)
	QStr = QStr & " 		ELSE	(T.SF_MIKTAR - T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - COALESCE(S01.SF_MIKTAR,0)) END"
	QStr = QStr & "  ELSE 0 END REZ_MIKTAR, "
	QStr = QStr & " CASE WHEN T.ARTNO='" & MPSSipArtNo & "' THEN 'E' ELSE 'H' END SELECTED,"
	QStr = QStr & " TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(T.REV_RTESTARIH, 'YYYY/MM/DD'),'DD/MM/YYYY') REV_RTESTARIH"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK40T T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT ALLOC_SIPARTNO, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK01 WHERE ALLOC_SYSSTATUS NOT IN (' ','3') GROUP BY ALLOC_SIPARTNO)"
	QStr = QStr & " 		S01 ON S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO=T.ARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=T.KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=T.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE T.KOD='" & STOK20T.KOD & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND T.ACIKKAPALI=' '"
	QStr = QStr & " AND (T.SF_MIKTAR - T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - COALESCE(S01.SF_MIKTAR,0)) > 0"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO, T.SRNUM"

'	Doc.d7MsgBox QStr, "", MB_OK

	Set MTableT1=Doc.RunSQLQuery("MAKROTABLET1", QStr)
	MTableT1.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_AV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub RezerveEt()
	Set STOK20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")

	Set MT1 = Doc.Select2("dr44t67", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sipariþ Seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if MT1.SumOf("REZ_MIKTAR") > MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(1) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Rezerve edilecek miktar stoktaki miktardan fazla olamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if MT1.SumOf("REZ_MIKTAR") = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Rezerve edilecek miktar girilmemiþ.", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if MT1.SumOf("REZ_MIKTAR") > MT1.SumOf("ACIK_MIKTAR") * 1.05 Then
		ret = Doc.d7MsgBox("Sipariþ miktarý : " & MT1.SumOf("ACIK_MIKTAR") & " m, Rezerve Edilecek Miktar : " & _
				MT1.SumOf("REZ_MIKTAR") & " m. Devam etmek istiyor musunuz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO+MB_ICONWARNING)
		if ret <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40", "")
	if Doc1 Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to MT1.GetRowCount()
		Call RezervasyonKaydet(Doc1, MT1, i)
	Next
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Function GetGuncelMakaraMiktari()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK20E) Then Set STOK20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK20T) Then Set STOK20T = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	QStr = "SELECT SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE KOD='" & STOK20T.KOD & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND LOTNUMBER='" & STOK20T.LOTNUMBER & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND SERINO='" & STOK20T.SERINO & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND VRI_VR_C='" & STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_C & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND VRI_VR_R1='" & STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND VRI_VR_R2='" & STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND VRI_VR_R3='" & STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND VRI_NUM1='" & STOK20T.SF_VRI_NUM1 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND VRI_NUM2='" & STOK20T.SF_VRI_NUM2 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND AMBCODE='" & STOK20E.AMBCODE & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND LOCATION1='" & STOK20T.LOCATION1 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND LOCATION2='" & STOK20T.LOCATION2 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND LOCATION3='" & STOK20T.LOCATION3 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND LOCATION4='" & STOK20T.LOCATION4 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND FOKOD='" & STOK20T.FOKOD & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sadgaerAJUE5JSFG", QStr)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Miktar = 0
	Else
		Miktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
	End if
	GetGuncelMakaraMiktari = Miktar
End Function
Sub RezervasyonKaydet(Doc1, MT, satirNo)
	ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(MT.EVRAKNO(satirNo))
	if ret <> 0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set STOK20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set S40T1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T1")
	
	Set S40T2 = Doc1.Select2("asdgqerhh", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & MT.ARTNO(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if S40T2.GetRowCount() <> 1 Then Exit Sub

	S40T.SetCurrentRow(Doc.Cdbl(S40T2.SRNUM(1)))

	sNo = S40T1.AddRow
	S40T1.SetCurrentRow(sNo)
	S40T1.KOD(sNo) = MT2.KOD(1)
	S40T1.LOTNUMBER(sNo) = MT2.LOTNUMBER(1)
	S40T1.AMBCODE(sNo) = STOK20E.AMBCODE
	S40T1.SF_MIKTAR(sNo) = MT.REZ_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	S40T1.FOKOD(sNo) = MT2.FOKOD(1)
	S40T1.LOCATION1(sNo) = MT2.LOCATION1(1)
	S40T1.LOCATION2(sNo) = MT2.LOCATION2(1)
	S40T1.LOCATION3(sNo) = MT2.LOCATION3(1)
	S40T1.LOCATION4(sNo) = MT2.LOCATION4(1)
	S40T1.SERINO(sNo) = MT2.SERINO(1)
	S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT(sNo) = MT2.SF_SF_UNIT(1)
	S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_C(sNo) = MT2.SF_VRI_VR_C(1)
	S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R1(sNo) = MT2.SF_VRI_VR_R1(1)
	S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R2(sNo) = MT2.SF_VRI_VR_R2(1)
	S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R3(sNo) = MT2.SF_VRI_VR_R3(1)
	S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM1(sNo) = MT2.SF_VRI_NUM1(1)
	S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM2(sNo) = MT2.SF_VRI_NUM2(1)
	
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_AV()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	if MTableT1.SELECTED = "E" Then
		Call OncekiSecimleriKaldir(MTableT1.ARTNO)
		
		RezMiktar = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(1)
		MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR = RezMiktar
	Else
		MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR = 0
	End if
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_AV2()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	if MTableT1.SELECTED = "E" Then
		Set MT1 = Doc.Select2("asdgDDerah", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "ARTNO<>'" & MTableT1.ARTNO & "'", -1)
		OncekiRez = 0
		if MT1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			OncekiRez = MT1.SumOf("REZ_MIKTAR")
		End if

		if (MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(1) - OncekiRez) > MTableT1.ACIK_MIKTAR Then
			RezMiktar = MTableT1.ACIK_MIKTAR
		Else
			RezMiktar = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(1)
		End if
		MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR = RezMiktar
	Else
		MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR = 0
	End if
End Sub
Sub OncekiSecimleriKaldir(ArtNo)
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	For i = 1 to MTableT1.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MTableT1.ARTNO(i)) <> trim(ArtNo) and trim(MTableT1.SELECTED(i)) = "E" Then
			MTableT1.SELECTED(i) = "H"
			MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR(i) = 0
		End if
	Next
	MTableT1.Refresh
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KODDUZELT1
Hangi Tablo için : MAKR7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kod Düzelt
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Kod = MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT
	KodArray = Split(Kod, vbcrlf)
	MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = ""
	Girinti = 0
	For i = 0 to uBound(KodArray)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & uBound(KodArray)
		satir = KodArray(i)
		satir = Replace(satir, Chr(9), " ")
		satir = trim(Satir)
				
		if uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SUB" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END FUNCTION" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END IF" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "NEXT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "WEND" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "LOOP" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SELECT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 2
		End if
		YeniSatir = Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
		
		MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = trim(MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT) & YeniSatir & vbcrlf
		'  MAKR7SE_B.SCRIPTTEXT = YeniSatir
		
		if Ucase(Left(satir, 4)) = "SUB " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif Ucase(Left(satir, 9)) = "FUNCTION " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "IF " and Not TekSatirIf(satir) Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 12)) = "SELECT CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 2
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 4)) = "FOR " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 6)) = "WHILE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "DO " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
	Girintilisatir = satir
	For i = 1 to Girinti
		Girintilisatir = Chr(9) & Girintilisatir
	Next
End Function
Function TekSatirIf(satir)
	TekSatirIf = False
	k = InStr(1, uCase(satir), "THEN ")
	if k = 0 Then Exit Function
	if trim(Mid(satir, k + 5)) <> "" Then
		TekSatirIf = True
	End if
End Function
Sub TabTrim(satir)
	While Left(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Mid(satir, 2)
	Wend
	While Right(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Left(satir, Len(satir) - 1)
	Wend
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO + <BOMREC_INPUTTYPE+ BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE")
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher 
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TOL
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call MPSMiktarlariniToleransKadarArttir()
End Sub
Sub MPSMiktarlariniToleransKadarArttir()
	If MMPS22E.READ_OPT = "1" Then Exit Sub
	Set f = doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")

	For i = 1 to M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetRowCount()
		if Left(M22_MMPS10E_LIST.MAMULSTOKKODU(i), 1) = "M" Then
			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetAppName()	
			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",i
			Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

			For j = 1 to M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.GetRowCount()
				Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.GetAppName()	
				Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",j
				Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

				ToleransTuru = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_TOLERANSTYPE
				if trim(ToleransTuru) = "-+" or trim(ToleransTuru) = "+" Then
					Tolerans = 1 + M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_TOLERANS / 100
					M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE = (M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_MIKTART - M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_NET_TRANSFER) * Tolerans
				End if
			Next
		End if
	Next
	Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetAppName()	
	Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",1
	Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S

Açýklama : Sevk Emri Oluþtur
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SIP", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub STOK01_Doldur()
	Set S40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPNO='" & S40E.EVRAKNO & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", -1)
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	MTableT.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()		
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For Each AlanAdi in Alanlar
			if AlanAdi <> "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				AlanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				MTableT.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set S40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf5r55yy7", "MAKROTABLET", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýr seçmediniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Eklenen = Eklenen + 1
			if Eklenen = 1 Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK30", "")
				Set S30E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30E")
				Set S30T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30T")
				Doc1.New_Voucher
				S30E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(i)
				S30T.Empty
			End if
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & Eklenen
			satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
			S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S30T.CH_KOD = S40E.MUSTERIKODU
			S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S40E.MUSTERIKODU, "")

			S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)

			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
			S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
			S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
			S30T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S30T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
			S30T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
			S30T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
			S30T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
			S30T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
			S30T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
			S30T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
			S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR	 = MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
			S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
		End if
	Next
	if Eklenen > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SEVKEMRI2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK30
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Sevk Emri Oluþtur
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
'	MTable.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SIP", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SEVKEMRIOPT", "CHAR",1,0,"R","",""
	MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT = "0"

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub STOK01_Doldur()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	SiparisNo = MTable.SIPNO
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("fsf3f3f", "STOK40E", "CHSIPNO='" & MTable.CHSIPNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S40E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			SiparisNo = S40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		End if
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", -1)
	Else
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPNO='" & SiparisNo & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", -1)
	End if
'	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	MTableT.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rezervasyonlar Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For Each AlanAdi in Alanlar
			if AlanAdi <> "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				AlanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				MTableT.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.MUSTERIKODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
		MTableT.MUSTERIKODU_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT.MUSTERIKODU, "")
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf5r55yy7", "MAKROTABLET", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýr seçmediniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S30E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK30E")
	Set S30T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK30T")

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Eklenen = Eklenen + 1
			if Eklenen = 1 and trim(MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT) = "0" Then
				Doc.New_Voucher
				S30E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
				S30T.Empty
			End if
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & Eklenen
			satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
			S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S30T.CH_KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
			S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S30T.CH_KOD, "")

			S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)

			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
			S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
			S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
			S30T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S30T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
			S30T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
			S30T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
			S30T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
			S30T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
			S30T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
			S30T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
			S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR	 = MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
			S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
		End if
	Next
	if Eklenen > 0 Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	'Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MUH-3
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üzerinde Bulunulan Satýrýn Mühendislik Tanýmýný Göster/Yeni Yarat
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE <> "I" Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Operasyon satýrýnýn üzerinde bulunmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	QStr = "KRITERCODE_1='SKOD'&KRITERVALUE_1='" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU &"'"
	QStr = QStr & "&KRITERCODE_2='OKOD'&KRITERVALUE_2='" & MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON &"'"
	Set T10 = Doc.Select1("dde4344r5", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("TECH10", "")
	Set T10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10E")
	Set T10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10T")

	if T10.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc1.New_Voucher()
		T10E.KRITERCODE_1 = "SKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_1 = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
		T10E.KRITERCODE_2 = "OKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_2 = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON
	Elseif T10.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO
	Else
		Str1 = "Bu ürün ve operasyona ait birden çok mühendislik tanýmý bulundu." & vcrlf & _
					"Ekrana getirmek istediðiniz mühendislik tanýmýný seçiniz" & vbcrlf
		For i = 1 to T10.GetRowCount()
			Str2 = i & ". Föy No : " & trim(T10.EVRAKNO(i)) 
			if (T10.KRITERCODE_3(i)) = "CKOD" Then
				Str2 = Str2 & ", Müþteri : " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(T10.KRITERVALUE_3(i)), 16), "")
			End if
			Str1 = Str1 & Str2 & vbcrlf
		Next
		k = InputBox(Str1, "freedom", 1)
		if CInt(k) > 0 and CInt(k) <= T10.GetRowCount() Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO(cInt(k))
		End if
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK00
Hangi Tablo için : STOK00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgsdfjsj", "SELECT KOD, AD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD LIKE 'M%' AND CODETYPE='D'")
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Kaydediliyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()

		ret = Doc.UFLD00_CreateEditTable("ENG_NAME","STOK00", S00.KOD(i))
		Set KTA = Doc.GetTableObject("ENG_NAME")
		Parantez = InStr(1, S00.AD(i), "(")
		if Parantez > 0 Then
			EnAd = Left(S00.AD(i), Parantez-1)
		Else
			EnAd = S00.AD(i)
		End if
		KTA.ENG_NAME = EnAd
		KTA.Exec "SAVE"
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub Makro1()
	Set S21T = Doc.GetTableObject("MULTILEVEL1")
'	Call SendTableToExcel(S21T)
	Call SendGridToExcel(S21T, "MULTILEVEL1", "Envanter Standart Ürün Aðaçlarý Analizi")
End Sub
Sub SendGridToExcel(Tablo, GridName, RaporAciklama)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	Set ReportOutput=Doc.CreateSObject("ReportOutput")

	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 RaporAciklama

	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", GridName)
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,1) > 0 Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			AlanBasligi = Grid(i,2)

			If AlanTuru = "N" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Grid(i,0), Grid(i,5), Grid(i,6), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanBasligi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & GridName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Grid,1)
			if Grid(i,1) > 0 Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
				AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
				If AlanTuru = "N" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "D" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	ExcelFile = PE2.GetFileName()
	pe2.Close
	Call ExcelFormat(ExcelFile, uBound(Grid,1) + 1)
End Sub

Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
End Function
Sub ExcelFormat(ExcelFile, AlanSayisi)
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open(ExcelFile)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	A1 = Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value
	B1 = Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value

	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value = ""
	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = ""

	Sh1.Range(Sh1.Cells(3, 	1), Sh1.Cells(3, AlanSayisi)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = True
	Excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 3
	Excel.Selection.Font.ColorIndex = 2
	Excel.Selection.EntireColumn.AutoFit

	
	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value = A1
	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = B1

	Excel.Cells(1,1).Select
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub

Sub Suz()

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty

	QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
				" FROM STOK00"  & _
				" WHERE 1=1"
	if trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
'		where upper(supplier_name) like ('TEST%');
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD1)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD1,"_"," "))) & "%'"

	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD2)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD2,"_"," "))) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD3)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD3,"_"," "))) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD4)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD4,"_"," "))) & "%'"
	End if

	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XXXX_1", QStr
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableobject("XXXX_1")

	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Listeye Ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTable2.AddRow()
		MTable2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTable2.KOD = S00.KOD(i)
		MTable2.AD = S00.AD(i)

		MTable2.T_TARIH = Doc.Bugun()

		MTable2.MA_STOK = Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(S00.KOD(i),"MA")

		MTable2.GK_1 = S00.GK_1(i)
		MTable2.GK_2 = S00.GK_2(i)
		MTable2.GK_3 = S00.GK_3(i)
		MTable2.GK_4 = S00.GK_4(i)
		MTable2.GK_5 = S00.GK_5(i)
		MTable2.GK_6 = S00.GK_6(i)
		MTable2.GK_7 = S00.GK_7(i)
		MTable2.GK_8 = S00.GK_8(i)
		MTable2.GK_9 = S00.GK_9(i)
		MTable2.GK_10 = S00.GK_10(i)
	Next
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Sub Sec()

	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	Set S46T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T")

	satirno =  S46T.GetRowCount()

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdfgsdfhsj", "MAKROTABLE2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to MT.GetRowCount() 			
		S46T.SetCurrentRow(satirno)
		S46T.KOD(satirno) = MT.KOD(i)
		S46T.RTESTARIH	(satirno) = MT.T_TARIH(i)
		satirno = S46T.AddRow()	
	Next 

	MTable2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTable2.empty
	MTable2.Refresh

	S46T.SetCurrentRow(1)

'	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

End Sub

Sub Suz()

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")

	MTable2.Empty

				" FROM STOK00"  & _
				" WHERE 1=1"
	if trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
'		where upper(supplier_name) like ('TEST%');
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD1)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD1,"_"," "))) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD2)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD2,"_"," "))) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD3)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD3,"_"," "))) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD4)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(rTrim(replace(MTable.AD4,"_"," "))) & "%'"
	End if

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("XXXX_1", QStr)
'MTable2.AppendTable(S00)
'exit sub

	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Listeye Ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTable2.AddRow()
		MTable2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTable2.KOD = S00.KOD(i)
		MTable2.AD = S00.AD(i)

		MTable2.T_TARIH = Doc.Bugun()

		MTable2.GK_1 = S00.GK_1(i)
		MTable2.GK_2 = S00.GK_2(i)
		MTable2.GK_3 = S00.GK_3(i)
		MTable2.GK_4 = S00.GK_4(i)
		MTable2.GK_5 = S00.GK_5(i)
		MTable2.GK_6 = S00.GK_6(i)
		MTable2.GK_7 = S00.GK_7(i)
		MTable2.GK_8 = S00.GK_8(i)
		MTable2.GK_9 = S00.GK_9(i)
		MTable2.GK_10 = S00.GK_10(i)
	Next
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Sub Sec()

	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	Set S47T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")

	satirno =  S47T.GetRowCount()

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdfgsdfhsj", "MAKROTABLE2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to MT.GetRowCount() 			
		S47T.SetCurrentRow(satirno)
		S47T.KOD(satirno) = MT.KOD(i)
		S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satirno) = MT.T_TARIH(i)
		satirno = S47T.AddRow()	
	Next 

	MTable2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTable2.empty
	MTable2.Refresh

	S47T.SetCurrentRow(1)

'	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ACILIS2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK2A
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Stok Açýlýþlarýný Excel 'den Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set gggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\acilis.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	colStokKodu = 4
	colLotNo = 7
	colSeriNo = 1
	colMiktar = 9
	colAmbalajNo = 8
	colAciklama = 20
	colBrutAgirlik = 21
	colNetAgirlik = 22
	colEskiKod = 5
	colSiparis = 17

	STOK2AT.Empty
	For i = 2 to 2923
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'den okunuyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,colStokKodu).Value
		'if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit For

		'StokKodu1 = GetFrStokKodu(StokKodu)
		StokKodu1 = StokKodu
		if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
			satirNo = STOK2AT.AddRow
			STOK2AT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			STOK2AT.KOD(satirNo) = StokKodu1
			STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,colMiktar).Value)
			AmbalajTuru = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colAmbalajNo).Value)
			SeriNo = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colSeriNo).Value)
			LotNo = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colLotNo).Value)
			Aciklama = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colAciklama).Value)
			EskiKod = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colEskiKod).Value)
			BrutAgirlik = Doc.Cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,colBrutAgirlik).Value)
			NetAgirlik = Doc.Cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,colNetAgirlik).Value)
			SiparisNo = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,colSiparis).Value)


			STOK2AT.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", satirNo, SeriNo
			if trim(AmbalajTuru) = "0000" and STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) <= 200 Then
				AmbalajTuru = ""
				NVaryant1 = Doc.Cdbl(Sh1.Cells(i,colMiktar).Value)
				SeriNo = ""
				LotNo = ""
			Else
				NVaryant1 = ""
			End if
			STOK2AT.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = AmbalajTuru
			STOK2AT.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = NVaryant1
			STOK2AT.SERINO(satirNo) = SeriNo
			STOK2AT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = LotNo
			STOK2AT.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = Aciklama
			STOK2AT.BRUT(satirNo) = BrutAgirlik
			STOK2AT.NET(satirNo) = NetAgirlik
			STOK2AT.ESKI_KOD(satirNo) = EskiKod
			STOK2AT.SIPARIS(satirNo) = SiparisNo
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr, Stok Kodu boþ", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GetFrStokKodu(StokKodu)
	GetFrStokKodu = ""
	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vdfssj", "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & trim(StokKodu) & "'")
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFrStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function

Makro Kodu : HESAPLA
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01X
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Hacimden aðýrlýk hesapla
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to BOMU01XT.GetRowCount()
		if BOMU01XT.REFNUM01(i) > 0 Then
			Call MiktarHesapla(i)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub MiktarHesapla(satirNo)
	Set BOMU01XT = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01XT")
	if trim(BOMU01XT.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo)) <> "H" Then Exit Sub
	if trim(BOMU01XT.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SEsdfshs", "SELECT B_YOGUNLUK FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & BOMU01XT.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) & "'")
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	if S00.B_YOGUNLUK(1) = 0 Then Exit Sub

	BOMU01XT.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = Doc.Round(BOMU01XT.REFNUM01(satirNo) * S00.B_YOGUNLUK(1),6)
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set STOK2AT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK2AT")
	STOK2AT.AddField2 "ACIKLAMA", "CHAR", 100,0,"L", "", ""
	STOK2AT.AddField2 "BRUT", "DOUBLE", 12,3,"R", "", ""
	STOK2AT.AddField2 "NET", "DOUBLE", 12,3,"R", "", ""
	STOK2AT.AddField2 "ESKI_KOD", "CHAR", 50,0,"L", "", ""
	STOK2AT.AddField2 "SIPARIS", "CHAR", 10,0,"R", "F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')", ""
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ESKI_KOD
Hangi Tablo için : STOK2A
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Eski Kodlarý Üretici Kodlarýna Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Dddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set S2AT = Doc.Select2G("sadfsdfhsj", "STOK2AT", "ESKI_KOD>'" & Space(50) & "'", "", "KOD",-1)
	if S2AT.GetRowCOunt() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set S2AT1 = Doc.Select2("sdfgdfjddddddsjk", S2AT.GetAppName, "ESKI_KOD='" & Space(50) & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S2AT1.GetRowCount()
		if i = 1 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Aþaðýdaki kartlara ait birden çok eski kod bulundu :", "", MB_OK
		End if
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(S2AT1.KOD(i)) & "   " & trim(S2AT1.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)), "", MB_OK
	Next
'	if S2AT1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
	Set S00 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
	For i = 1 to S2AT.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S2AT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S2AT.ESKI_KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher S2AT.KOD(i)
			S00.SUPPLIERCODE = S2AT.ESKI_KOD(i)
			Doc1.Save_Voucher
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK00", ""
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : REZERVE1
Hangi Tablo için : STOK2A
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Açýlýþ Fiþinden Sipariþ Rezervasyonlarýný Oluþtur
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set S2AT = Doc.Select2("zdfgdshsj", "STOK2AT", "SIPARIS>'" & Space(10) & "'", "", -1)
	if S2AT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set gggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK32", "")
	Set STOK32E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK32E")
	Set STOK32T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK32T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	STOK32E.AMBCODE = STOK2AE.AMBCODE
	STOK32T.Empty
	For i = 1 to S2AT.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S2AT.GetRowCount()
		
		SipArtNo = GetSipArtNo(S2AT.KOD(i), S2AT.SIPARIS(i))

		if SipArtNo <> "" Then
			satirNo = STOK32T.AddRow
			STOK32T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	
			STOK32T.KOD(satirNo) = S2AT.KOD(i)
			STOK32T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = S2AT.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			STOK32T.SF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = S2AT.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
			STOK32T.SERINO(satirNo) = S2AT.SERINO(i)
			STOK32T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = S2AT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			STOK32T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S2AT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			STOK32T.FARKLIDAGITIM(satirNo) = "*"
			STOK32T.REAL_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = SipArtNo
			STOK32T.REAL_SIPNO(satirNo) = S2AT.SIPARIS(i)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
'	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetSipArtNo(StokKodu, SiparisNo)
	GetSipArtNo = ""

	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM STOK40E"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo), 10) & "'"
	Set S40E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdesak", QStr)
	if S40E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sipariþ No : " & trim(SiparisNo) & " no.lu sipariþ bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT ARTNO FROM STOK40T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo), 10) & "' AND KOD='" & StokKodu & "'"
	Set S40T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdesak", QStr)
	if S40T.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		GetSipArtNo = S40T.ARTNO(1)
	Elseif S40T.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & StokKodu & ", Sipariþ No : " & trim(SiparisNo) & " birden fazla satýrda bulundu", "", MB_OK
		GetSipArtNo = S40T.ARTNO(1)
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & StokKodu & ", " & trim(SiparisNo) & " no. lu sipariþte bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Function

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Çeki Listesini Sevk Emrine Dönüþtür
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call SevkEmriOlustur()
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("wef3fsdf3wfwsd", "STOK01", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='CL'&ALLOC_EVRAKNO='" & STOK66E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu Çeki Listesi daha önce sevk emrine dönüþtürülmüþ", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK30", "")
	Set S30E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30E")
	Set S30T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30T")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S30E.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(1)
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i
		satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
		S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S30T.CH_KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
		S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S30T.CH_KOD, "")
		
		S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = S01.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		
		Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
		S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
		S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
		S30T.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
'		S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(i), "")
		S30T.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S30T.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		S30T.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		S30T.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		S30T.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		S30T.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		S30T.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		S30T.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
		S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR  = S01.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
		S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S01.KOD(i), "")
		S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
		S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
	Next
		STOK66E.NOTES_4 = trim(S30E.EVRAKNO)
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(S30E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Sevk Emri yaratýldý", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sevk Emri kaydedilemedi", "Ýþlem Baþarýsýz", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BOMREC
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Excel den Ürün Aðacý Oluþturan Makro
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

'''*************Excel Dökümanýnýn Adresini bu alanda belirtin********************

	Set Dosya = Excel.Workbooks.Open("C:\Users\ibrahim\Desktop\WORKBOOK\UNTEL\XDESIGN.xls")

'''*********************************

	Set Sh1 = Dosya.Sheets(1)
	
	Set T_BOMU01E = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
	Set T_BOMU01T = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
	T_BOMU01T.SafePack()
	
	Dim i,kk,t

	for kk = 1 to 256
		nret = Doc.New_Voucher()
		mamulkod = trim(Sh1.Cells(1,kk).value)
		markalama = trim(Sh1.Cells(2,kk).value)

		ret = Doc.UFLD00_CreateEditTable("xxx","STOK00",mamulkod)
		Set KTA = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx")

		KTA.MARKALAMA = trim(markalama)
		KTA.Exec "SAVE"

		T_BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = mamulkod

		i=5
		While trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk).value) <> ""
			sirano = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk).value)
			kartturu = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk+1).value)
			kaynakkodu = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk+2).value)
			operasyon = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk+4).value)
			refnum = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk+6).value)
			hmmiktar = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk+7).value)
			pkno = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk+9).value)
			ymmiktar = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,kk+10).value)
	
			t = T_BOMU01T.AddRow()
		
			T_BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(t)
			T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO(t) = sirano
			T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(t) = kartturu 
			T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(t) = kaynakkodu 
			T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON(t) = operasyon 
			T_BOMU01T.REFNUM01(t) = refnum 
			T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(t) = hmmiktar 
			T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(t) = pkno 
			T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(t) = ymmiktar 
			i = i + 1
			
		Wend
	
		Doc.Save_Voucher

		kk = kk + 15
	Next
		Dosya.close
		Excel.Quit
	
		Set excel = nothing
		Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	
End Sub

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
		Set S00 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")
		Set S0U = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK0U")
		For i = 1 to STOK0GT.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK0GT.GetRowCount()
			if trim(STOK0GT.STOKCODE(i)) <> "" Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK0GT.STOKCODE(i)

				satirNo = S0U.AddRow()
				S0U.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

				S0U.UCODE(satirno) = STOK0GT.MSTOKCODE(i)

				Doc1.Save_Voucher
			End if
		Next
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MUH-1
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üzerinde Bulunulan Satýrýn Mühendislik Tanýmýný Göster/Yeni Yarat
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE <> "I" Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Operasyon satýrýnýn üzerinde bulunmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	QStr = "KRITERCODE_1='SKOD'&KRITERVALUE_1='" & BOMU01E.MAMULCODE &"'"
	QStr = QStr & "&KRITERCODE_2='OKOD'&KRITERVALUE_2='" & BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON &"'"
	Set T10 = Doc.Select1("dde4344r5", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("TECH10", "")
	Set T10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10E")
	Set T10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10T")

	if T10.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc1.New_Voucher()
		T10E.KRITERCODE_1 = "SKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_1 = BOMU01E.MAMULCODE
		T10E.KRITERCODE_2 = "OKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_2 = BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON
	Elseif T10.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO
	Else
		Str1 = "Bu ürün ve operasyona ait birden çok mühendislik tanýmý bulundu." & vcrlf & _
					"Ekrana getirmek istediðiniz mühendislik tanýmýný seçiniz" & vbcrlf
		For i = 1 to T10.GetRowCount()
			Str2 = i & ". Föy No : " & trim(T10.EVRAKNO(i)) 
			if (T10.KRITERCODE_3(i)) = "CKOD" Then
				Str2 = Str2 & ", Müþteri : " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(T10.KRITERVALUE_3(i)), 16), "")
			End if
			Str1 = Str1 & Str2 & vbcrlf
		Next
		k = InputBox(Str1, "freedom", 1)
		if CInt(k) > 0 and CInt(k) <= T10.GetRowCount() Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO(cInt(k))
		End if
	End if
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE", "CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT1
	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "FOKOD","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_C","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_R1","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_R2","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_R3","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_NUM1","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_NUM2","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "REZ_MIKTAR","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "REZ_MIKTAR_AV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	MTable.AMBCODE = "MA"
	Call RezerveveStokDetaylariniGetir()
End Sub
Sub RezerveveStokDetaylariniGetir()
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	StokKodu = S40T.KOD
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Call SiprisListesiniGetir()
	Call DetayStokListesiniGetir()
End Sub
Sub SiprisListesiniGetir()
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	Set S40T1 = Doc.Select2("dde34r55", "STOK40T1", "", "", -1)
	MTableT1.Empty()
	if S40T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to S40T1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rezervasyonlar Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S40T1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow()
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT1.SELECTED = "E"
		MTableT1.KOD = S40T1.KOD(i)
		MTableT1.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT1.KOD, "")
		MTableT1.LOTNUMBER = S40T1.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT1.SERINO = S40T1.SERINO(i)
		MTableT1.AMBCODE = S40T1.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT1.SF_MIKTAR = S40T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT1.SF_SF_UNIT = S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR = S40T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT1.FOKOD = S40T1.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_C = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION1 = S40T1.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION2 = S40T1.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION3 = S40T1.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION4 = S40T1.LOCATION4(i)
	Next
	MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub DetayStokListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	QStr = "KOD='" & S40T.KOD & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then 
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", QStr, "KOD", -1)
	MTableT2.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT2.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT2.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT2.KOD, "")
		MTableT2.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT2.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT2.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
	Next
	MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
End Sub
Sub RezervasyonlariKaydet()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set S40T1 = Doc.GetTableobject("STOK40T1")
	Set MT1 = Doc.Select2("dr44t67", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("dr44t67sd", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)

	S40T1.EmptyFiltered
	For i = 1 to MT1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S40T1.AddRow
		S40T1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S40T1.KOD = MT1.KOD(i)
		S40T1.AMBCODE = MT1.AMBCODE(i)
		S40T1.SF_MIKTAR = MT1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT = MT1.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		S40T1.LOTNUMBER = MT1.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S40T1.SERINO = MT1.SERINO(i)
		S40T1.FOKOD = MT1.FOKOD(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_C = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MT1.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MT1.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION1 = MT1.LOCATION1(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION2 = MT1.LOCATION2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION3 = MT1.LOCATION3(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION4 = MT1.LOCATION4(i)
	Next

	For i = 1 to MT2.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S40T1.AddRow
		S40T1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S40T1.KOD = MT2.KOD(i)
		S40T1.AMBCODE = MT2.AMBCODE(i)
		S40T1.SF_MIKTAR = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR(i)
		S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT = MT2.SF_UNIT(i)
		S40T1.LOTNUMBER = MT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S40T1.SERINO = MT2.SERINO(i)
		S40T1.FOKOD = MT2.FOKOD(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_C = MT2.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MT2.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MT2.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MT2.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MT2.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MT2.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION1 = MT2.LOCATION1(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION2 = MT2.LOCATION2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION3 = MT2.LOCATION3(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION4 = MT2.LOCATION4(i)
	Next

	Doc.Save_Voucher()
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT2.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub
Sub REZ_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("ee4r56", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MT2.GroupBy("KOD")
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK_KAR
Hangi Tablo için : MAKR7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set gggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\acilis.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	colStokKodu = 5
	colStokAdi = 6

	For i = 2 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'den okunuyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,colStokKodu).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit For
		StokAdi = Sh1.Cells(i,colStokAdi).Value
		Call Temizle(StokAdi)
		if trim(StokAdi) <> "" Then
			QStr1 = "'%" & Replace(StokAdi, " ", "%%") & "%'"
			QStr = "SELECT KOD, AD FROM STOK00 WHERE AD LIKE " & QStr1
			Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdgsedjs", QStr)
			if S00.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
				Sh1.Cells(i, 26).Value = S00.KOD(1)
				Sh1.Cells(i, 27).Value = S00.AD(1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub
Sub Temizle(Str1)
	Str1 = trim(Str1)
	Do
		if InStr(1, Str1, "  ") = 0 Then Exit Do
		Str1 = Replace(Str1, "  ", " ")
	Loop
End Sub

Sub Hesapla()
	Call DetayListHesapla()
End Sub
Sub DetayListHesapla()
	Set gg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set KRT = Doc.GetTableObject("KRITERLER")

	QStr = "SELECT MMPS10T.EVRAKNO, MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU, STOK00.AD KAYNAK_AD, STOK00.IUNIT IUNIT, MMPS10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK,"
	QStr = QStr & " MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, S01.STOK_MIKTAR REZERVE_STOK, (MMPS10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK - COALESCE(S01.STOK_MIKTAR,0)) REZ_BAKIYE,"
	QStr = QStr & " (MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR - MMPS10E.SF_URETILEN) MAMUL_MIKTAR, S00.AD MAMUL_AD, MMPS10E.SF_SF_UNIT MAMUL_UNIT, "
	QStr = QStr & " MMPS10T.R_STATUS, MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU, MMPS10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH,"
	QStr = QStr & " (CASE " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.BASLAMISMPS10='1' THEN 0 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='R' THEN 1 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='P' THEN 2 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='C' THEN 3 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='Q' THEN 4 " & _
								"ELSE 5 END) MPS_SIRA"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T"
	QStr = QStr & " INNER JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT ALLOC_MPSARTNO, Sum(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR FROM STOK01 WHERE ALLOC_MPSARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "' GROUP BY ALLOC_MPSARTNO) S01 ON MMPS10T.JOBNO=S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10E ON MMPS10E.EVRAKNO=MMPS10T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00 ON S00.KOD=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU"
	if not Doc.Is_EY1(KRT.YM_KONTROL) Then
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN BOMU01TM ON BOMU01TM.MAMULCODE=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU AND BOMU01TM.AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMU01TM.VARSAYILAN10='1'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MMPS10T.ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
	if not Doc.Is_EY1(KRT.YM_KONTROL) Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND (BOMU01TM.EVRAKNO IS NULL OR STOK00.KART_TIPI='F')"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU>='" & KRT.SKODB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.SKODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU<='" & KRT.SKODE & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND " & GetGrupKoduQStr
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU, MPS_SIRA, MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH"
	Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfw3efsssd", QStr)


	QStr = "SELECT STOK01.KOD, Sum(STOK01.STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN GDEF00 ON GDEF00.KOD=STOK01.AMBCODE"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE GDEF00.MRPDEYOK10<>'1' AND ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.KOD>='" & KRT.SKODB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.SKODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.KOD<='" & KRT.SKODE & "'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " AND " & GetGrupKoduQStr
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK01.KOD"
	Set S01S = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdf34fdfd", QStr)

	Set HLIST = Doc.GetTableObject("HMD_LIST")
	Set HOZET = Doc.GetTableObject("HMD_OZET")

	HLIST.Empty
	HOZET.Empty

	HLIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	HOZET.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	EskiStokKodu = ""
	satirNo1 = 0
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & M10T.GetRowCount()
		if M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) <> EskiStokKodu Then
			Set S01S1 = Doc.Select2("sdf3wefsdf", S01S.GetAppName, "KOD='" & M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if S01S1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				StokMiktari = S01S1.STOK_MIKTAR(1)
			Else
				StokMiktari = 0
			End if
			KalanMiktar = StokMiktari
			satirNo1 = HOZET.AddRow
			HOZET.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo1) = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			HOZET.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo1) = M10T.KAYNAK_AD(i)
			HOZET.IUNIT(satirNo1) = M10T.IUNIT(i)
			HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) = StokMiktari
			HOZET.SERBEST_STOK(satirNo1) = StokMiktari
			HOZET.ILK_IHTIYAC_TARIHI(satirNo1) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)

		satirNo = HLIST.AddRow
		HLIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = M10T.EVRAKNO(i)
		HLIST.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		HLIST.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo) = M10T.KAYNAK_AD(i)
		HLIST.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)
		HLIST.IUNIT(satirNo) = M10T.IUNIT(i)
		HLIST.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(satirNo) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
		HLIST.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo) = M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HLIST.REZ_BAKIYE(satirNo) = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i)
		HLIST.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = M10T.R_MAMULKODU(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_AD(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_UNIT(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_UNIT(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
		HLIST.R_STATUS(satirNo) = M10T.R_STATUS(i)
		HLIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = M10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)

		if M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) > 0 and KalanMiktar > 0 Then
			if KalanMiktar < M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) Then
				Karsilanan = KalanMiktar
			Else
				Karsilanan = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i)
			End if
			KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - Karsilanan
		Else
			Karsilanan = 0
		End if
		HLIST.STOKLA_KARSILANAN(satirNo) = Karsilanan
		HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) - Karsilanan
		HLIST.STOKTA_KALAN(satirNo) = KalanMiktar

		HOZET.TOPLAM_IHTIYAC(satirNo1) = HOZET.TOPLAM_IHTIYAC(satirNo1) + M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)
		HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) = HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) + M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HOZET.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo1) = HOZET.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo1) + M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HOZET.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo1) = HOZET.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo1) + HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo)
		HOZET.IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
		if HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = 0 Then
			HOZET.KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
		Else
			HOZET.HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
			if trim(HOZET.ILK_TALEP_TARIHI(satirNo1)) = "" Then
				HOZET.ILK_TALEP_TARIHI(satirNo1) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next

	Call GetTumMPSList()

	HLIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	HOZET.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub GetUretilebilenList()
	Set MDLIST = Doc.Select2("MPS_SUB_LST", "MPS_LIST", "URT_STATU='E'", "", -1)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing

Sub GetTumMPSList()
	Set HL = Doc.Select2("asdfd2wfwsdf", "HMD_LIST", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	Set MLIST = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_LIST")
	MLIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	MLIST.Empty

	For i = 1 to HL.GetRowCount()
		doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'lerin Üretilebilirlik Durumlarý Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & HL.GetRowCount()
		if HL.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiMPSNo Then
			satirNo = MLIST.AddRow
			MLIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = HL.EVRAKNO(i)
			MLIST.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = HL.R_MAMULKODU(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_AD(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_UNIT(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_UNIT(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
			MLIST.R_STATUS(satirNo) = HL.R_STATUS(i)
			MLIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = HL.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
			MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
			MLIST.URT_BAKIYE(satirNo) = 0
			MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "E"
		End if
		EskiMPSNo = HL.EVRAKNO(i)
		UretilebilecekMik = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(HL.REZERVE_STOK(i) + HL.STOKLA_KARSILANAN(i), HL.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)) * HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i), 3)
		if UretilebilecekMik < MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) Then
			MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = UretilebilecekMik
			MLIST.URT_BAKIYE(satirNo) = MLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) - UretilebilecekMik
			if MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = 0 Then
				MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "H"
			Else
				MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "K"
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MLIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Function GetGrupKoduQStr()
	Set KRT = Doc.GetTableObject("KRITERLER")
	QStr = ""
	QStr = QStr & " STOK00.GK_1>='" & KRT.GK_1B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_1E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_1<='" & KRT.GK_1E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_2>='" & KRT.GK_2B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_2E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_2<='" & KRT.GK_2E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_3>='" & KRT.GK_3B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_3E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_3<='" & KRT.GK_3E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_4>='" & KRT.GK_4B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_4E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_4<='" & KRT.GK_4E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_5>='" & KRT.GK_5B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_5E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_5<='" & KRT.GK_5E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_6>='" & KRT.GK_6B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_6E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_6<='" & KRT.GK_6E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_7>='" & KRT.GK_7B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_7E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_7<='" & KRT.GK_7E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_8>='" & KRT.GK_8B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_8E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_8<='" & KRT.GK_8E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_9>='" & KRT.GK_9B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_9E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_9<='" & KRT.GK_9E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_10>='" & KRT.GK_10B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_10E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_10<='" & KRT.GK_10E & "'"
	End if
	GetGrupKoduQStr = QStr
End Function

TAB "MPS 'ler"
 GROUPBOX T="" AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=1 TOP=+1 WIDTH=1007p HEIGHT=50p TXTCOL=RGB_000_089_132
