Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ZER-024
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : LÝSTE SAYFASINDAKÝ ÝRSALÝYELERÝ LÝNK 'E GÖNDER
Kod : 
Sub Makro1
	if AktarimOncesiKontrollerdeHata() Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		if trim(LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)) <> "" Then
			Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
			Call Makro2()
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub Makro2()
	if trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye Numarasý Girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("dsfgedhsj", "STOK60T", "", "", -1)
	S60T.GroupBy2("KOD+SIPARTNO")

	Set ofs = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	saat = Doc.Saat(6)
	strXmlFile  = "Z:\IRSALIYELER\AKTARILACAK\" & trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO) & ".xml"

	Set objXMLFile = ofs.CreateTextFile(strXmlFile)

'	Set objXMLFile = ofs.OpenTextFile(strXmlFile, 2, True, 0)

	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<?xml version=""1.0"" encoding=""windows-1254"" ?>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<SatisFisi xmlns:xsi="& Chr(34) &"http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"& Chr(34) &" xmlns:xsd="& Chr(34) &"http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema"& Chr(34) &">"

	fisno = STOK60E.EVRAKNO
	tarih_saat = Replace(STOK60E.TARIH, "/", "-") & "T00:00:00"
	irsaliyeno = trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO)
	depokodu = trim(STOK60E.AMBCODE)
	depo = trim(Doc.GetDBfieldValue("GDEF00", "AD", depokodu , ""))
	Doviz = trim(STOK60K.OR_PB(1))
	kur = trim(STOK60K.OR_KUR(1))
	firmakodu = trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU)
	firmaadi = trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD)
	firmamuhasebekodu = trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU)
	sevkadresi = ""
	fiilisevkiyattarihi = Replace(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH, "/", "-") & "T00:00:00"
	plakano = trim(STOK60E.BOX1_3)
	nakliyeci = trim(STOK60E.BOX1_1)
	ozelkodu1 = trim(STOK60E.NOTES_2)
	ozelkodu2 = trim(STOK60E.NOTES_3)
	malzemetipi = "2"

	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<FisNo>" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & "</FisNo>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<Tarih>" & tarih_saat&"</Tarih>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<IrsaliyeNo>" & trim(STOK60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO) & "</IrsaliyeNo>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<Depo>" & depo & "</Depo>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<DepoKodu>" & depokodu & "</DepoKodu>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<Doviz>" & Doviz & "</Doviz>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<Kur>" & kur & "</Kur>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<FirmaKodu>" & firmakodu & "</FirmaKodu>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<FirmaAdi>" & firmaadi & "</FirmaAdi>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<FirmaMuhasebeKodu>" & firmamuhasebekodu & "</FirmaMuhasebeKodu>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<SevkAdresi />"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<FiiliSevkTarihi>" & fiilisevkiyattarihi & "</FiiliSevkTarihi>"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<PlakaNo />"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<Nakliyeci />"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<OzelKodu1 />"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<OzelKodu2 />"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<Aciklama />"
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<MalzemeFisTipi>" & malzemetipi & "</MalzemeFisTipi>"

	S60T.SafePack()
	for i = 1 To S60T.GetRecCount()	
		QStr = "SELECT OR_FIYAT, TL_FIYAT, PRICEUNIT, ISKONTO_EFEKTIF"
		QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK40T"
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE ARTNO='" & S60T.SIPARTNO(i) & "'"
		Set S40T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdfsj", QStr)

		adres = ""
		siparisno = trim(S60T.SIPNO(i))
		malzemekodu = trim(S60T.KOD(i))
		malzemeadi = trim(S60T.KOD_STOK00_AD(i))
		malzememuhasebekodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TSENO", S60T.KOD(i), "")
		lotno =	trim(S60T.LOTNUMBER(i))
		miktar = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		birim =	trim(S60T.SF_SF_UNIT(i))

		if S40T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Set S60T1 = Doc.Select2("sdsdsj", "STOK60T", "KOD='" & S60T.KOD(i) & "'", "", -1)
			birimfiyat =	S60T1.TL_FIYAT(1)
			tutari = S60T1.TL_TUTAR(1)
			dovizbirimfiyat = S60T1.OR_FIYAT(1)
			kdvOrani = S60T1.KDV_ORANI(1)
			iskontoOrani = 0
		Else

			birimfiyat = S40T.TL_FIYAT(1)
			tutari = S40T.TL_FIYAT(1) * miktar
			dovizbirimfiyat = S40T.OR_FIYAT(1)

'				kdvOrani = S40T.KDV_ORANI(1)
				kdvOrani = 18
				iskontoOrani = S40T.ISKONTO_EFEKTIF(1)
		End if

		ozelkodu1 = ""
		ozelkodu2 = ""
		aciklama = ""
		listefiyati = ""
		malzemeid = ""

	  	objXMLFile.WriteLine "<SatisFisDetay>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<Adres />"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<SiparisNo>" & siparisno & "</SiparisNo>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<MalzemeKodu>" & malzemekodu & "</MalzemeKodu>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<MalzemeAdi>" & malzemeadi & "</MalzemeAdi>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<MalzemeMuhasebeKodu>" & malzememuhasebekodu & "</MalzemeMuhasebeKodu>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<LotNo />"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<Miktari>" & miktar & "</Miktari>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<Birimi>" & birim & "</Birimi>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<BirimFiyati>" & birimfiyat & "</BirimFiyati>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<Tutari>" & tutari & "</Tutari>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<DovizliBirimFiyati>" & dovizbirimfiyat & "</DovizliBirimFiyati>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<KDV>" & kdvOrani & "</KDV>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<OzelKodu1 />"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<OzelKodu2 />"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<Aciklama />"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<Iskonto>" & iskontoOrani & "</Iskonto>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<ListeFiyati>" & listefiyati & "</ListeFiyati>"
	    	objXMLFile.WriteLine "	<MalzemeID>" & malzemeid & "</MalzemeID>"
	  	objXMLFile.WriteLine "</SatisFisDetay>"
	next
	
	objXMLFile.WriteLine "</SatisFisi>"
	objXMLFile.Close
	Set oFS = Nothing
End Sub
Function AktarimOncesiKontrollerdeHata()
	AktarimOncesiKontrollerdeHata = False
	Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
		InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & LISTET.EVRAKNO(i) & "'"
	Next

	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM STOK60E"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE EVRAKNO IN (" & InStr1 & ")"
	QStr = QStr & " AND OR_IRSALIYESIRANO='" & Space(20) & "'"
	Set S60E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zdfgsdfhs", Qstr)
	For i = 1 to S60E.GetRowCount()
		if i = 1 Then Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye Seri No 'su boþ olan irsaliyeler : ", "", MB_OK
		Doc.d7MsgBox S60E.EVRAKNO(i), "", MB_OK
		AktarimOncesiKontrollerdeHata = True
	Next

	QStr = "SELECT STOK60T.KOD, STOK00.AD FROM STOK60T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK60T.KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE EVRAKNO IN (" & InStr1 & ")"
	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.TSENO='" & Space(24) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK60T.KOD, STOK00.AD"

	Set S60T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zdfgsdfhs", Qstr)
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		if i = 1 Then Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok kartýnda TSE No 'su boþ olan stok kartlarý : ", "", MB_OK
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(S60T.KOD(i)) & "  " & S60T.AD(i), "", MB_OK
		AktarimOncesiKontrollerdeHata = True
	Next
End Function

Sub R_TRANSFER_DIKKATE_ALINSIN_AfterValidate()
	Set M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST")
	ToleransTuru = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_TOLERANSTYPE
	if trim(ToleransTuru) = "-+" or trim(ToleransTuru) = "+" Then
		Tolerans = 1 + M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_TOLERANS / 100
		M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE = Doc.Round((M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_MIKTART - M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_NET_TRANSFER) * Tolerans,0)
	End if	
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE", "CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MUSTERIADI", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT1
	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SEVK_ADRESI","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "NETAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "BRUTAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "HACIM","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""

	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")

	Dim Par

	MTable.AMBCODE = "MA"
	'Call RezervasyonlarveCekiListeleri()
End Sub
Sub RezervasyonlarveCekiListeleri()
	Call DetayStokListesiniGetir()
	Call CekiListeleriniGetir()
End Sub
Sub CekiListeleriniGetir()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT1.Empty()

	QStr = "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='CL'"
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("dde34rs55", "STOK01", QStr, "", "ALLOC_EVRAKNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow()
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT1.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT1.EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT1.MUSTERI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK66E", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i), "")
		MTableT1.MUSTERI_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT1.MUSTERI, "")
		Set S66T = Doc.Select1G("wef44dfwe42s", "STOK66T", "EVRAKNO='" & S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i) & "'", "", "OALLOC_SIPNO", -1)
		SipNo = ""
		For j = 1 to S66T.GetRowCount()
			if trim(S66T.OALLOC_SIPNO(j)) <> "" Then
				SipNo = SipNo & ", " & trim(S66T.OALLOC_SIPNO(j))
			End if
		Next
		if trim(SipNo) <> "" Then
			MTableT1.SIPARIS_NO = Mid(SipNo, 3)
		End if
		S66T.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
		MTableT1.BRUTAGIRLIK = S66T.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTableT1.NETAGIRLIK =  S66T.NETAGIRLIK
		MTableT1.HACIM = S66T.HACIM
	Next
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub DetayStokListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	QStr = "SELECT STOK01.*, STOK00.AD STOK_ADI, (STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR) NET_AGIRLIK, STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU,"
	QStr = QStr & " CARI00.AD MUSTERI_ADI, STOK40E.CHSIPNO, STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH,"
	QStr = QStr & " A00.AGIRLIK AMBALAJ_AG, A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_DISGENISLIK / 1000000000 HACIM"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK40E ON STOK40E.EVRAKNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 ON CARI00.KOD=STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN AMBLJ00 A00 ON A00.KOD=STOK01.VRI_VR_R1"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE<>'CL' AND STOK01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE<>'SV' AND STOK01.ALLOC_SIPNO<>'          '"
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then 
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.MUSTERIADI) <> "" Then
		if Doc.IsSQL Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(CARI00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.MUSTERIADI)) & "%'"
		Else
			QStr = QStr & " AND UCASE(CARI00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.MUSTERIADI)) & "%'"
		End if
	End if
	if trim(MTable.SIPNO) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SIPNO ='" & MTable.SIPNO & "'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.CHSIPNO) <> "" Then
		if Doc.IsSQL Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK40E.CHSIPNO) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.CHSIPNO)) & "%'"
		Else
			QStr = QStr & " AND UCASE(STOK40E.CHSIPNO) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.CHSIPNO)) & "%'"
		End if
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY STOK01.KOD"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dde34r55", QStr)

	MTableT2.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT2.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT2.KOD_STOK00_AD = S01.STOK_ADI(i)
		MTableT2.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT2.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT2.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		MTableT2.MUSTERI = S01.MUSTERIKODU(i)
		MTableT2.MUSTERI_AD = S01.MUSTERI_ADI(i)
		MTableT2.REV_RTESTARIH = S01.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		MTableT2.CHSIPNO = S01.CHSIPNO(i)




'30-1-12		MTableT2.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1 = S01.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1(i)

		MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK = S01.NET_AGIRLIK(i)

		MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK = MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK + S01.AMBALAJ_AG(i)

		MTableT2.HACIM = S01.HACIM(i)
'		MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK = Doc.Round(GetNetAgirlik(MTableT2.KOD(i), MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)), 0)
'		MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK= DOC.ceil(MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK) 
 '   MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK= ROUND(MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK/5)*5 
	Next
'	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
'	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
'		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Brüt Aðýrlýk ve Hacim Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & MTableT2.GetRowCount()
'		Call GetAmbalajAgirligi_Hacim(MTableT2, i, AmbalajAgirligi, Hacim)
'		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(i)
'		MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK = MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK + AmbalajAgirligi
'		MTableT2.HACIM = Hacim
'	Next

	MTableT2.Sort("MUSTERI_AD")
	MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = 0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = 0
End Sub
Sub YeniCekiListesiYarat()
	Call CekiListesineEkle("")
End Sub
Sub SecilenCekiListesineEkle()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.Select2("wer23rrtddd", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Eklenecek çeki listesi seçmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	Elseif MTableT1.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yalnýzca 1 adet çeki listesi seçebilirsiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Call CekiListesineEkle(MTableT1.EVRAKNO(1))
End Sub
Sub CekiListesineEkle(CekiListesiNo)
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.Select2("wer23rrtd", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MTableT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK66", "")
	Set S66E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK66E")
	Set S66T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK66T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")

	if trim(CekiListesiNo) = "" Then
		Doc1.New_Voucher
		S66E.MUSTERIKODU = MTableT2.MUSTERI
		S66E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
	Else
		Doc1.Load_Voucher CekiListesiNo
	End if

	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT2.SELECTED(i) = "E" and Trim(MTableT2.DURUM(i)) <> "Aktarýldý" Then
			satirNo = S66T.AddRow()
			S66T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S66T.KOD = MTableT2.KOD(i)
			S66T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S66T.SERINO = MTableT2.SERINO(i)
			S66T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S66T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT2.SF_UNIT(i)
			S66T.NETAGIRLIK = MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK(i)
			S66T.BRUTAGIRLIK = MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)
			S66T.HACIM = MTableT2.HACIM(i)
			S66T.LOCATION1 = MTableT2.LOCATION1(i)
			S66T.LOCATION2 = MTableT2.LOCATION2(i)
			S66T.LOCATION3 = MTableT2.LOCATION3(i)
			S66T.LOCATION4 = MTableT2.LOCATION4(i)
			S66T.FOKOD = MTableT2.FOKOD(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT2.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT2.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S66T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT2.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S66T.SIPNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S66T.SIPARTNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT2.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT2.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S66T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
			S66T.REF_TEXT_1 = MTableT2.CHSIPNO(i)
			S66T.REF_TEXT_2 = MTableT2.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1(i)
		End if
	Next
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
		'Call EkraniTazele()
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT2.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT + MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM + MTableT2.HACIM
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT - MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM - MTableT2.HACIM
	End if
End Sub
Sub REZ_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("ee4r56", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MT2.GroupBy("KOD")
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR
End Sub
Function GetNetAgirlik(StokKodu, Miktar)
	GetNetAgirlik = 0
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("we3fsddsf", "MAKROTABLET2", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&NETAGIRLIK>0", "", 1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Div(MT2.NETAGIRLIK * Miktar, MT2.SF_MIKTAR)
	Else
		GetNetAgirlik = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG", StokKodu, Miktar)
	End if
End Function
Sub GetAmbalajAgirligi_Hacim(MTableT2, satirNo, AmbalajAgirligi, Hacim)
	AmbalajAgirligi = 0
	Hacim = 0

	MakaraTipi = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1(satirNo)
		if trim(MakaraTipi) <> "" and trim(MakaraTipi) <> "0000" then
'			Set A00 = Doc.Select1("Asd2q3dsaf", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='" & Doc.LeftJustify(MakaraTipi,24) & "'", "", -1)
			Set A00 = Doc.Select1("Asd2q3dsaf", "AMBLJ00", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(MakaraTipi,24) & "'", "", -1)
			if A00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				AmbalajAgirligi = A00.AGIRLIK
				Hacim = A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_DISGENISLIK / 1000000000
			Else
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Ambalaj Tanýmlarý 'nda " & MakaraTipi & " makaraya ait ambalaj tanýmý bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			End if
		End if

End Sub
Sub EkraniTazele()
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("wef23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED<>'E'", "", -1)
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty
	
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Alanlar = MTableT2.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MT2.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			AlanDegeri = MT2.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT2.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
		Next
	Next
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK002
Hangi Tablo için : STOK00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Grup Kodlarýný Kullanarak Ýletken yapýsýný Hesaplan Gk_2 bazýnda
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	ilgili_grup_kodu = Inputbox("Revizyon Etmek istediðiniz Kodlarýn Gk_2 deðerini Giriniz","Freedom")
	ilgili_grup_kodu = trim(ilgili_grup_kodu)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00","")
	Set T_S00 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK00")

	Set S00 = Doc1.RunSQLQuery("sdfg", "SELECT KOD, AD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD LIKE 'M%' AND CODETYPE='D' AND GK_2='"& ilgili_grup_kodu &"'")
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.StatusBarMessage "Kaydediliyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.Load_Voucher trim(S00.KOD(i))

		GK3 = Doc1.GetGecoValue("STKGK3","AD",T_S00.GK_3,-1)
		GK4 = Doc1.GetGecoValue("STKGK4","AD",T_S00.GK_4,-1)

		GK3 = Doc.Cdbl(replace(GK3,",","."))
		GK4 = Doc.Cdbl(replace(GK4,",","."))

		T_S00.B_B_TECH_1 = GK3 * GK4
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD3","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD4","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD2_1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""

	Dim MTable2
	Set MTable2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	MTable2.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "NAME2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "T_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "MA_STOK","DOUBLE",15,2,"R","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "MA_REZV","DOUBLE",15,2,"R","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "MA_SERB","DOUBLE",15,2,"R","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_1","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_2","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_3","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_4","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_5","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_6","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_7","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_8","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_9","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "GK_10","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""

	MTable2.AddReportFields()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

End Sub
Sub Suz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty

	QStr = "SELECT *"  & _
				" FROM STOK00"  & _
				" WHERE 1=1"
	if trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
'		where upper(supplier_name) like ('TEST%');
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD1)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD1),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD2)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD2),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD3)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD3),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
'		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(trim(MTable.AD4)) & "%'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & ucase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD4),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if

	Doc.RunSQLQuery "XXXX_1", QStr
	Set S00 = Doc.GetTableobject("XXXX_1")

	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Listeye Ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTable2.AddRow()
		MTable2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTable2.KOD = S00.KOD(i)
		MTable2.AD = S00.AD(i)
		MTable2.NAME2 = S00.NAME2(i)

		MTable2.T_TARIH = Doc.Bugun()

		MTable2.MA_STOK = Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(S00.KOD(i),"MA")
		MTable2.MA_REZV = Doc.Stok00c_SonRezerve(S00.KOD(i),"MA","")
		MTable2.MA_SERB = Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(S00.KOD(i),"MA","")

		MTable2.GK_1 = S00.GK_1(i)
		MTable2.GK_2 = S00.GK_2(i)
		MTable2.GK_3 = S00.GK_3(i)
		MTable2.GK_4 = S00.GK_4(i)
		MTable2.GK_5 = S00.GK_5(i)
		MTable2.GK_6 = S00.GK_6(i)
		MTable2.GK_7 = S00.GK_7(i)
		MTable2.GK_8 = S00.GK_8(i)
		MTable2.GK_9 = S00.GK_9(i)
		MTable2.GK_10 = S00.GK_10(i)
	Next
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Sub Sec()
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	satirno =  S40T.GetRowCount()
	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdfgsdfhsj", "MAKROTABLE2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to MT.GetRowCount() 			
		S40T.SetCurrentRow(satirno)
		S40T.KOD(satirno) = MT.KOD(i)
		S40T.RTESTARIH	(satirno) = MT.T_TARIH(i)
		satirno = S40T.AddRow()	
	Next 
	MTable2.empty
	S40T.SetCurrentRow(1)
'	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub

Function GetHammaddeHarcamaListesi(BaglantiliMPSList, MPSNo)
	TableStr = "MPS_HMD_LIST__" & GetRndString()
	Doc.CreateTableObject TableStr & "=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject(TableStr)
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETSAYISI", "DOUBLE", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAK_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_KAYNAKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_KAYNAKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_MIKTAR0", "DOUBLE", 12, 3, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_TRANSFERMIK", "DOUBLE", 12, 3, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddReportFields
	
	BaglantiliMPSList.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select1G("fsdff4fsg", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	ToplamUretim = M10TM1.SF_URETILEN(1)
	ToplamMiktar = M10TM1.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	Oran = Doc.Div(ToplamUretim, ToplamMiktar)

	For i = 1 to BaglantiliMPSList.GetRowCount()
		Set M10T = Doc.Select1("fsfwssf4s", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & BaglantiliMPSList.EVRAKNO(i) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
		For j = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
			Qstr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP'&KAYNAKMPS='" & M10T.EVRAKNO(j) & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & M10T.TRNUM(j) & "'"
			Set MLx1 = Doc.Select2("wedwfdrtetfg", BaglantiliMPSList.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)
			if MLx1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then

				satirNo = ML.AddRow()
				ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAKMPS(i)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i)
				ML.EVRAKNO = BaglantiliMPSList.EVRAKNO(i)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = BaglantiliMPSList.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = BaglantiliMPSList.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(i)
				ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_PAKETSAYISI(i)
				ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_PAKETICERIGI(i)
				ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i)
				ML.SF_SF_UNIT = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
				ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(i)
				ML.MPS_TRNUM = BaglantiliMPSList.MPS_TRNUM(i)
				ML.R_KAYNAKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(j)
				ML.R_KAYNAKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.R_KAYNAKKODU, "")
				ML.R_MIKTAR0 = M10T.R_MIKTAR0(j) * Doc.Div(BaglantiliMPSList.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(i)) * Oran
				NetTransfer = M10T.R_HM_ODONEMNET(j) + M10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN(j) - M10T.R_HM_IADE_EDILEN(j)
				ML.R_TRANSFERMIK = NetTransfer * Doc.Div(BaglantiliMPSList.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(i))
			End if
		Next
	Next
	Set GetHammaddeHarcamaListesi = ML
	Doc.DeleteTableObject MLx1.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName()
End Function
Function GetKaynakMPSList(MpsNo, MpsTRNUM)
	TableStr = "__MPS_BLST" & GetRndString()
	Doc.CreateTableObject TableStr & "=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject(TableStr)
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "r", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETSAYISI", "DOUBLE", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAK_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "ORAN", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MUSTERISIPINO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MUSTERISIPIARTNO", "CHAR", 16, 0, "R", "", ""

	ML.AddReportFields
	
	ML.Empty
	Set GetKaynakMPSList = ML
	
	QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & MpsNo & "'"
	if trim(MpsTRNUM) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&TRNUM='" & MpsTRNUM & "'"
	End if
	' Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("ee4f5454t", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	
	satir = M10TM.GetCurrentRow()
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		siraNo = ML.AddRow()
		ML.SetCurrentRow(siraNo)
		ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		ML.KAYNAKMPS = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i)
		ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i)
		ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)
		ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
		ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
		ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI(i)
		ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI(i)
		ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i)
		ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM.EVRAKNO(i), "")
		ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		ML.MUSTERISIPINO = M10TM.MUSTERISIPINO(i)
		ML.MUSTERISIPIARTNO = M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i)
		Oran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR)
		ML.ORAN = Oran
	Next
	Call KaynaklariHesapla(ML)
	Set GetKaynakMPSList = ML

	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM.GetAppName
End Function
Sub KaynaklariHesapla(ML)
	siraNo = 1
	Do
		if ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(siraNo) = "UP" Then
			Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dr5t678ji", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & ML.KAYNAKMPS(siraNo) & "'&TRNUM='" & ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(siraNo) & "'", "", -1)
			if M10T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select1("dee44x", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
				'KaynakOran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(siraNo), ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(siraNo))
				KaynakOran = ML.ORAN(siraNo)
				For j = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
					Set MLXX = Doc.Select2G("wef4fwfwdf", ML.GetAppName, "KAYNAKMPS='" & ML.KAYNAKMPS(siraNo) & "'", "", "KAYNAKMPS", -1)
					KaynakMPSToplamMiktar = MLXX.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
					if ListedeYok(ML, M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j), M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS(j), M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j)) Then
						satirNo1 = ML.AddRow()
						
						ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
						ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j)
						ML.KAYNAKMPS = M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS(j)
						ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j)
						ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM1.EVRAKNO(j)
						ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10TM1.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
						ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
						ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = M10TM1.SF_PAKETSAYISI(j)
						ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10TM1.SF_PAKETICERIGI(j) '* Oran
						ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI * ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI
						ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM1.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
						ML.MUSTERISIPINO = M10TM1.MUSTERISIPINO(j)
						ML.MUSTERISIPIARTNO = M10TM1.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(j)
						ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM1.EVRAKNO, "")
'if trim(ML.EVRAKNO) = "2570" Then msgbox KaynakOran
'						ML.ORAN = KaynakOran * Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR) * 
						ML.ORAN = KaynakOran * Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR) * Doc.Div(MLXX.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(siraNo))
					End if
				Next
			End if
		End if
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		if siraNo > ML.GetRowCount Then Exit Do
		if siraNo = 150 Then Exit Do
	Loop
	if IsObject(M10T) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T.GetAppName
	if IsObject(M10TM1) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName
	if IsObject(MLXX) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject MLXX.GetAppName
End Sub
Function ListedeYok(ML, KaynakEvrakType, KaynakEvrakNo, KaynakTrNum)
	ListedeYok = True
	QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='" & KaynakEvrakType & "'&KAYNAKMPS='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & KaynakTrNum & "'"
	Set ML1 = Doc.Select2("f455ff33gfg", ML.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)
	if ML1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then ListedeYok = False
	Doc.DeleteTableObject ML1.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetBaglantiliMPSList1(KaynakEvraktype, KaynakEvrakNo, KaynakTRNum)
	Set GetBaglantiliMPSList1 = GetBaglantiliMPSList("UP", "xyz11_", "000000")
	Set tbl = Doc.Select1("dee44XF4", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='__KXXX__'", "", -1)

	if KaynakEvraktype = "UP" Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'"
		if trim(KaynakTRNum) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & "&TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
		End if
		Set tbl = Doc.Select1("dee44XF4", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	Elseif KaynakEvraktype = "SP" Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'"
		if trim(KaynakTRNum) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & "&TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
		End if
		Set tbl = Doc.Select1("dee44XF4", "STOK40T", QStr, "", -1)
	End if
	For i = 1 to tbl.GetRowCount()
		Set BL1 = GetBaglantiliMPSList(KaynakEvraktype, tbl.EVRAKNO(i), tbl.TRNUM(i))
		GetBaglantiliMPSList1.AppendTable(BL1)
		Doc.DeleteTableObject BL1.GetAppName
	Next
	Doc.DeleteTableObject tbl.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetBaglantiliMPSList(KaynakEvraktype, KaynakEvrakNo, KaynakTRNum)
	TableStr = "MPS_BGL_LIST__" & GetRndString()
	Doc.CreateTableObject TableStr & "=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject(TableStr)
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "r", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETSAYISI", "DOUBLE", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAK_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddReportFields
	
	Set GetBaglantiliMPSList = ML
	if KaynakEvraktype = "UP" Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
	Else
		QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='" & KaynakEvraktype & "'&KAYNAKMPS='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
	End if
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dee44", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	siraNo = ML.AddRow()
	ML.SetCurrentRow(siraNo)
	ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE
	ML.KAYNAKMPS = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS
	ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM
	ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO
	ML.MPS_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM
	ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU
	ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = 1
	ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
	ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
	ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT
	ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM.EVRAKNO, "")
	Oran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR)
	
	Do
		Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dr5t678ji", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & ML.EVRAKNO(siraNo) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
		For j = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
			KaynakEvraktype = "UP"
			KaynakEvrakNo = ML.EVRAKNO(siraNo)
			KaynakTRNum = M10T.TRNUM(j)
			QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='" & KaynakEvraktype & "'&KAYNAKMPS='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
			Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select1("dee44x", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
			if M10TM1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				satirNo1 = ML.AddRow()
				ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = KaynakEvrakType
				ML.KAYNAKMPS = KaynakEvrakNo
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = KaynakTRNum
				ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM1.EVRAKNO(1)
				ML.MPS_TRNUM = M10TM1.TRNUM(1)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(j)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
				ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = 1
				ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10T.R_MIKTART(j) * Oran
				ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10T.R_MIKTART(j) * Oran
				ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM1.SF_SF_UNIT
				ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM1.EVRAKNO, "")
			End if
		Next
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		if siraNo > ML.GetRowCount Then Exit Do
		Oran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(siraNo), ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(siraNo))
		if siraNo = 250 Then Exit Do
	Loop
	Set GetBaglantiliMPSList = ML
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName()
End Function
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName
	
	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut
	
	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gÃ¶nderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues
		
		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK00_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK00C_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_IMLT00_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_IMLT01_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_CARI00_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_MMPS10T_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK40T_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_MMPS10E_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPORT_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
End Function
Function GetRndString()
	GetRndString = CStr(Doc.Round(Doc.CDbl((Timer + Rnd) * 10000000), 0)) 
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	'Msgbox Calc_OpKG("32520300601003", "AMB", 2500)	
	Msgbox Calc_KG("32520300601003", 2500)	
'	MsgBox GetSayimSonucAgirlik(Doc.RightJustify("30/06/2014", 10))

Function Calc_KG(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Calc_KG = 0
	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if UCase(trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), ""))) = "KG" Then
				Calc_KG = Calc_KG + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function Calc_OpKG(StokKodu, OperasyonKodu, Miktar)
	Calc_OpKG = 0
	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	OpBulundu = False
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("1","M", StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys1 = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.Select2("srf4fghhy7u6urtu", Tbmrsys1.GetAppName, "", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "I" and not OpBulundu Then
			if trim(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_OPERASYON(i)) = trim(OperasyonKodu) Then OpBulundu = true
			QStr = "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_SIRANO='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_SIRANO(i) & "'"
			Set B0 = Doc.Select2("fseffssvy", Tbmrsys.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)
			For j = 1 to B0.GetRowCount()
				if UCase(trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", B0.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j), ""))) = "KG" Then
					Calc_OpKG = Calc_OpKG + B0.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j)
				Else
					Calc_OpKG = Calc_OpKG + Calc_KG(B0.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j), B0.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(j))					
				End if
			Next
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function Calc_Cu_KG(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Calc_Cu_KG = 0
	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if UCase(trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), ""))) = "KG" Then
'				if Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), 3) = "101" Then
				if Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), 6) > "HM0200" and Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <= "HM0300" Then
					Calc_Cu_KG = Calc_Cu_KG + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function Calc_kg_ve_Cu_kg(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Dim AgirlikArr(1)
	AgirlikArr(0) = 0
	AgirlikArr(1) = 0
	Calc_kg_ve_Cu_kg = AgirlikArr

	if Miktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("O","M", StokKodu,1,Miktar,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			if UCase(trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), ""))) = "KG" Then
				if Left(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), 3) = "101" Then
					AgirlikArr(1) = AgirlikArr(1) + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				End if
			End if
			AgirlikArr(0) = AgirlikArr(0) + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
		End if
	Next
	Calc_kg_ve_Cu_kg = AgirlikArr
End Function
Function GetSayimSonucAgirlik(SayimEvrakStr)
	GetSayimSonucAgirlik = 0
	SayimArr = Split(SayimEvrakStr, ",")
	For Each SayimEvrakNo in SayimArr
		SayimEvrakNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(SayimEvrakNo), 10)
		if trim(SayimEvrakNo) <> "" Then
			GetSayimSonucAgirlik = GetSayimSonucAgirlik + GetSayimAgirlik(SayimEvrakNo)
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetSayimAgirlik(SayimEvrakNo)
	GetSayimAgirlik = 0
	Set S11T = Doc.Select1G("d4fwef3ffsdf4fwef", "SAYM11T", "EVRAKNO='" & SayimEvrakNo & "'&KOD>='2'", "", "KOD+SF_VRI_VR_R3",-1)
	For i = 1 to S11T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sayým Sonucu Aðýrlýklarý Hesaplanýyor : " & trim(SayimEvrakNo) & " : " & i & " / " & S11T.GetRowCount()
		GetSayimAgirlik = GetSayimAgirlik + Calc_KG2(S11T.KOD(i), Left(S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i), 6), S11T.SF_MIKTAR(i))
	Next
End Function
Function Calc_KG2(StokKodu, Op, Miktar)
	Calc_KG2 = 0
	if Left(StokKodu, 1) = "2" Then
		Calc_KG2 = Calc_KG(StokKodu, Miktar)
	Else
		if trim(Op) <> "" Then
			Calc_KG2 = Calc_OpKG(StokKodu, Op, Miktar)
		End if
	End if
End Function

Function GetBrutAgirlik(StokKodu, Miktar, SeriNo, MakaraTipi, PaletOp)
	GetBrutAgirlik = 0
	BirimAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_AGIRLIK", StokKodu, "")
	GetBrutAgirlik = BirimAgirlik * Miktar

'	Set S60T1 = Doc.Select2G("CFRGssg3hdfh3h", "STOK60T", "SERINO>='K'&SERINO<='KZZZZ'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if Left(SeriNo, 1) = "T" Then
		GetBrutAgirlik = GetBrutAgirlik + GetMakaraAgirligi(SeriNo)
	Elseif trim(MakaraTipi) <> "" Then
		GetBrutAgirlik = GetBrutAgirlik + GetMakaraAgirligi2(MakaraTipi, Miktar, StokKodu)
	Elseif Left(SeriNo, 1) = "K" and trim(PaletOp) <> "H" Then
		PaletIciMetre = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
		if PaletIciMetre = 0 Then
'			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & StokKodu & ", Stok kartýnda palet içi metraj tanýmlanmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		End if
		PaletSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(Miktar, PaletIciMetre))
		PaletAgirligi = 20 * PaletSayisi
		GetBrutAgirlik = GetBrutAgirlik + PaletAgirligi
	End if
End Function
Function GetMakaraAgirligi(SeriNo)
	GetMakaraAgirligi = 0
	if Left(SeriNo, 1) = "T" Then
		Bolu = InStr(1, SeriNo, "/")
		if Bolu < 4 Then Exit Function
		FlansCapi = Doc.Cdbl(Mid(SeriNo, 2, Bolu - 2)) * 10
		Set A00 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3gwdgsdg", "AMBLJ00", "KOD>='T'&KOD<='Tzzzzzz'&MKR_FLANSCAPI='" & FlansCapi & "'", "", -1)
		if A00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetMakaraAgirligi = A00.MKR_CITASIZDARA
		End if
	End if
End Function
Function GetMakaraAgirligi2(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, KabloKodu)
	KabloCapi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_CAP", KabloKodu, "")
	BirimKabloAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_AGIRLIK", KabloKodu, "")
	GetMakaraAgirligi2 = 0
	MakaraKodu = Doc.RunMacro5("MAKR7S.MAKARA", "GetMakaraAmbalajKodu", MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, KabloCapi, BirimKabloAgirligi, KabloKodu)
	if trim(MakaraKodu) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetMakaraAgirligi2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("AMBLJ00", "MKR_CITASIZDARA", MakaraKodu, "")
End Function
Function GetBrutAgirlik2(Arr1)
	StokKodu = Arr1(0)
	Miktar = Arr1(1)
	SevkBoyu = Arr1(2)
	SeriNo = Arr1(3)
	MakaraTipi = Arr1(4)
	PaletOp = Arr1(5)
	GetBrutAgirlik2 = GetBrutAgirlik1(StokKodu, Miktar, SevkBoyu, SeriNo, MakaraTipi, PaletOp)
End Function
Function GetBrutAgirlik1(StokKodu, Miktar, ByVal SevkBoyu, SeriNo, MakaraTipi, PaletOp)
	GetBrutAgirlik1 = 0
	BirimAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_AGIRLIK", StokKodu, "")
	GetBrutAgirlik1 = BirimAgirlik * Miktar

	if Left(SeriNo, 1) = "T" Then
		GetBrutAgirlik1 = GetBrutAgirlik1 + GetMakaraAgirligi(SeriNo)
	Elseif trim(MakaraTipi) <> "" Then
		if Miktar < SevkBoyu Then SevkBoyu = Miktar
		MakaraSayisi = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(Miktar, SevkBoyu), 0)
		if MakaraSayisi = 0 Then MakaraSayisi = 1
		GetBrutAgirlik1 = GetBrutAgirlik1 + MakaraSayisi * GetMakaraAgirligi2(MakaraTipi, SevkBoyu, StokKodu)
	Elseif Left(SeriNo, 1) = "K" and trim(PaletOp) <> "H" Then
		PaletIciMetre = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
		if PaletIciMetre = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & StokKodu & ", Stok kartýnda palet içi metraj tanýmlanmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		End if
		PaletSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(Miktar, PaletIciMetre))
		PaletAgirligi = 20 * PaletSayisi
		GetBrutAgirlik1 = GetBrutAgirlik1 + PaletAgirligi
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
'	Set T20T2 = Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL20T2")

Function GetMakaraAmbalajKodu(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, KabloCapi, BirimKabloAgirligi, KabloKodu)
	GetMakaraAmbalajKodu = ""

	if KabloCapi = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kartýnda Kablo Çapý Girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if
	Set MakaraTable = Doc.Select1("XXX", "AMBLJ00", "MKR_TURU='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MakaraTipi),10) & "'", "", -1)
	MakaraTable.Sort("MKR_ICHACIM")

	'MakaraTasima = TasimaSecim(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, BirimKabloAgirligi, MakaraTable)
	MakaraGobek = GobekSecim(MakaraTipi, KabloKodu, KabloCapi,MakaraTable)
	'MSGBOX MakaraGobek(1)

	MakaraKapasite = KapasiteSecim3(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, KabloCapi,MakaraTable)


'  MakaraHacim = MakaraTasima(0)
'  MakaraKodu = MakaraTasima(1)
	MakaraHacim = 0
	MakaraKodu = ""

  If MakaraGobek(0) > MakaraHacim Then
		MakaraHacim = MakaraGobek(0)
  		MakaraKodu = MakaraGobek(1)
  End If
  If MakaraKapasite(0) > MakaraHacim Then
		MakaraHacim = MakaraKapasite(0)
  		MakaraKodu = MakaraKapasite(1)
  End If
	GetMakaraAmbalajKodu = MakaraKodu
End Function
Function TasimaSecim(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, BirimKabloAgirligi, MakaraTable)
	Dim KabloAgirligi, TasimaKap,i,k
	KabloAgirligi = KabloUzunlugu * BirimKabloAgirligi
		
	Dim TasimaArr(1)
	TasimaArr(0) = 0
	TasimaArr(1) = ""
	TasimaSecim = TasimaArr	

	if BirimKabloAgirligi = 0 Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 To MakaraTable.GetRecCount()
		TasimaKap = MakaraTable.MKR_TASIMAKAP(i)
		If TasimaKap >= KabloAgirligi Then
			TasimaArr(0) = MakaraTable.MKR_ICHACIM(i)
			TasimaArr(1) = MakaraTable.KOD(i)
      	TasimaSecim = TasimaArr
      	Exit Function
		End If
	Next
	If TasimaArr(1) = "" Then
		k = Doc.d7msgbox("Bu kablo aðýrlýðýný taþýyabilecek makara bulunamadý","dinamo",MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
	End If
End Function
Function GobekSecim2(MakaraTipi, KabloKodu, KabloCapi, MakaraTable)	
	Gk4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_4", KabloKodu, "")
	Gk6 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_6", KabloKodu, "")
'	QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & KabloKodu & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE>='104'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE<='104zz'"
'	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("ded444t65", "BOMU01T", QStr, "", -1)
	If B01T.GetRowCount() > 0  Then 'zýrhlý
		GobekCapi = KabloCapi * 15
	Else
		GobekCapi = KabloCapi * 12
	End If
	If GobekSecim2 = 0 Then
		GobekSecim2 = GobekBul(MakaraTipi, GobekCapi, MakaraTable)
	End If
End Function
Function GobekSecim(MakaraTipi, KabloKodu, KabloCapi, MakaraTable)	
	Dim MakaraArr(1)
	MakaraArr(0) = 0
	MakaraArr(1) = ""

	if Left(KabloKodu, 3) = "M03" Then
		Set A00 = Doc.Select1("afgq34hd", "AMBLJ00", "MKR_TURU='" & MakaraTipi & "'&MKR_FLANSCAPI=1300", "", -1)
		if A00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			MakaraArr(0) = A00.MKR_ICHACIM
			MakaraArr(1) = A00.KOD
		End if
	End if
	GobekSecim = MakaraArr
End Function
Function GobekSecim2(MakaraTipi, KabloKodu, KabloCapi, MakaraTable)	
	Gk4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_4", KabloKodu, "")
	Gk6 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_6", KabloKodu, "")
	If trim(Gk6) = "Sektör"  Then
		GobekCapi = KabloCapi * 16
	Else
		GkAd2 = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK4", "AD_L2", Gk4, -1)
		if trim(GkAd2) = "TESISAT" Then
			GobekCapi = KabloCapi * 10
		Else
			GobekCapi = KabloCapi * 12
		End if
	End If
	GobekSecim2 = GobekBul(MakaraTipi, GobekCapi, MakaraTable)
End Function
Function GobekBul(MakaraTipi, GobekCapi, MakaraTable)
	Dim MakaraGobek,i,k
	Dim MakaraArr(1)
	GobekBul = MakaraArr
	For i = 1 To MakaraTable.GetRecCount()
		MakaraGobek = MakaraTable.MKR_GOBEKCAPI(i)
		If MakaraGobek >= GobekCapi Then
			MakaraArr(0) = MakaraTable.MKR_ICHACIM(i)
			MakaraArr(1) = MakaraTable.KOD(i)
			GobekBul = MakaraArr
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
	If MakaraArr(1) = "" Then
		k = Doc.d7msgbox("Göbek çapý bu çaptaki kabloyu sarmaya uygun makara bulunamadý","dinamo",MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
	End If
End Function
Function KapasiteSecim(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, KabloCapi, MakaraTable)
	Dim MakaraArr(1)
	MakaraArr(0) = 0
	MakaraArr(1) = ""
	KapasiteSecim = MakaraArr

	Dim Pi, FlansCapi, GobekCapi, IcGenislik, YanYana, UstUste, KapamaPayi, FlansYuksekligi, Kapasite,i,k
	Pi = 3.141592654
	
	For i = 1 To MakaraTable.GetRecCount()

		If Kapasite >= KabloUzunlugu Then
'			KapasiteSecim = FlansCapi
			MakaraArr(0) = MakaraTable.MKR_ICHACIM(i)
			MakaraArr(1) = MakaraTable.KOD(i)
			KapasiteSecim = MakaraArr
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
	If MakaraArr(1) = "" Then
		k = Doc.d7msgbox("Bu kablonun sýðabileceði makara bulunamadý","dinamo",MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
	End If
End Function
Function KapasiteSecim2(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, KabloCapi, MakaraTable)
	Dim Pi, FlansCapi, GobekCapi, IcGenislik, YanYana, UstUste, KapamaPayi, FlansYuksekligi, Kapasite,i,k
	Pi = 3.141592654
	Dim MakaraArr(1)
	MakaraArr(0) = 0
	MakaraArr(1) = ""
	KapasiteSecim2 = MakaraArr
	
	For i = 1 To MakaraTable.GetRecCount()
		FlansCapi = MakaraTable.MKR_FLANSCAPI(i)
		GobekCapi = MakaraTable.MKR_GOBEKCAPI(i)
		IcGenislik = MakaraTable.MKR_ICGENISLIK(i)
		BoslukFaktoru = 0.1
		TraversAraligi = KabloCapi * (1+BoslukFaktoru)
    
		YanYana = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(IcGenislik,TraversAraligi))
                 
		If KabloCapi <= 10 Then
			KapamaPayi = 30
		ElseIf KabloCapi <= 20 Then
			KapamaPayi = 75
		ElseIf KabloCapi <= 30 Then
			KapamaPayi = 85
		ElseIf KabloCapi <= 40 Then
			KapamaPayi = 100
		Else
			KapamaPayi = 120
		End If
'*******************
		KapamaPayi = 75
'*******************
		FlansYuksekligi = ((FlansCapi - GobekCapi) / 2) - KapamaPayi
		UstUste = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(FlansYuksekligi,KabloCapi))
		Kapasite = Round(YanYana * UstUste * ((GobekCapi + FlansCapi)/2 - KapamaPayi) * Pi / 1000, 0)
		If Kapasite >= KabloUzunlugu Then
'			KapasiteSecim2 = FlansCapi
'			KapasiteSecim2 = MakaraTable.KOD(i)
'			KapasiteSecim2 = Kapasite
			MakaraArr(0) = MakaraTable.MKR_ICHACIM(i)
			MakaraArr(1) = MakaraTable.KOD(i)
			KapasiteSecim2 = MakaraArr
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
	If MakaraArr(1) = "" Then
		k = Doc.d7msgbox("Bu kablonun sýðabileceði makara bulunamadý","dinamo",MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
	End If
End Function
Function KapasiteSecim3(MakaraTipi, KabloUzunlugu, KabloCapi, MakaraTable)
	Dim MakaraArr(1)
	MakaraArr(0) = 0
	MakaraArr(1) = ""
	KapasiteSecim3 = MakaraArr
	
	For i = 1 To MakaraTable.GetRecCount()
		Kapasite = GetMakaraKapasite(KabloCapi, MakaraTable.MKR_FLANSCAPI(i), MakaraTable.MKR_GOBEKCAPI(i), MakaraTable.MKR_ICGENISLIK(i))
'MSGBOX MakaraTable.MKR_FLANSCAPI(i) & ", " & Kapasite
		If Kapasite >= KabloUzunlugu Then
			MakaraArr(0) = MakaraTable.MKR_ICHACIM(i)
			MakaraArr(1) = MakaraTable.KOD(i)
			KapasiteSecim3 = MakaraArr
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
	If MakaraArr(1) = "" Then
		k = Doc.d7msgbox("Bu kablonun sýðabileceði makara bulunamadý","dinamo",MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
	End If
End Function
Function GetMakaraKapasite(KabloCapi, FlansCapi,GobekCapi, IcGenislik)
	Pi = 3.141592654
	BoslukFaktoru = 0.05
	TraversAraligi = KabloCapi * BoslukFaktoru
	TraversAdimi = KabloCapi + TraversAraligi
	DolguFaktoru = GetDolguFaktoru(BoslukFaktoru)    
	YanYana = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(IcGenislik,TraversAdimi))
	If KabloCapi <= 10 Then
		KapamaPayi = 30
	ElseIf KabloCapi <= 20 Then
		KapamaPayi = 75
	ElseIf KabloCapi <= 30 Then
		KapamaPayi = 85
	ElseIf KabloCapi <= 40 Then
		KapamaPayi = 100
	Else
		KapamaPayi = 120
	End If
'*******************
'	KapamaPayi = 75
'*******************
	If KabloCapi <= 21 Then
		KapamaPayi = 50
	ElseIf KabloCapi <= 40 Then
		KapamaPayi = 70
	Else
		KapamaPayi = 100
	End If

	FlansYuksekligi = ((FlansCapi - GobekCapi) / 2) - KapamaPayi
	UstUste = 1 + Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(FlansYuksekligi - KabloCapi, KabloCapi * (1 - DolguFaktoru)))
	GetMakaraKapasite = Round(YanYana * UstUste * (GobekCapi + KabloCapi * UstUste) * Pi / 1000, 0)
End Function
Function GetImalatMakaraKapasite(KabloCapi, FlansCapi,GobekCapi, IcGenislik)
	Pi = 3.141592654
	BoslukFaktoru = 0.05
	TraversAraligi = KabloCapi * BoslukFaktoru
	TraversAdimi = KabloCapi + TraversAraligi
	DolguFaktoru = GetDolguFaktoru(BoslukFaktoru)    
	YanYana = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(IcGenislik,TraversAdimi))
	If KabloCapi <= 10 Then
		KapamaPayi = 30
	ElseIf KabloCapi <= 20 Then
		KapamaPayi = 75
	ElseIf KabloCapi <= 30 Then
		KapamaPayi = 85
	ElseIf KabloCapi <= 40 Then
		KapamaPayi = 100
	Else
		KapamaPayi = 120
	End If
'*******************
	KapamaPayi = 30
'*******************

	FlansYuksekligi = ((FlansCapi - GobekCapi) / 2) - KapamaPayi
	UstUste = 1 + Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(FlansYuksekligi - KabloCapi, KabloCapi * (1 - DolguFaktoru)))
	GetImalatMakaraKapasite = Round(YanYana * UstUste * (GobekCapi + KabloCapi * UstUste) * Pi / 1000, 0)
End Function
Function GetDolguFaktoru(BoslukFaktoru)
	GetDolguFaktoru = 0
	if BoslukFaktoru < 0.05 Then
		GetDolguFaktoru = 0.134
	Elseif BoslukFaktoru < 0.1 Then
		GetDolguFaktoru = 0.149
	Elseif BoslukFaktoru < 0.15 Then
		GetDolguFaktoru = 0.165
	Elseif BoslukFaktoru < 0.2 Then
		GetDolguFaktoru = 0.182
	Else
		GetDolguFaktoru = 0.2
	End if
End Function
Function GetKapamaTahtasiInfo(MakaraKodu, TahtaKalinligi_cm, TahtaEni_cm, KapamaYuzdesi)
	Dim KapamaTahtasiInfo(1)
	KapamaTahtasiInfo(0) = 0
	KapamaTahtasiInfo(1) = ""

	YanakCapi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("AMBLJ00", "MKR_FLANSCAPI", MakaraKodu, "") / 1000
	MakaraDisGenislik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("AMBLJ00", "MKR_DISGENISLIK", MakaraKodu, "") / 1000

	pi = 3.141592654
'	TahtaSayisi = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(pi * (YanakCapi + TahtaKalinligi_cm / 100), TahtaEni_cm / 100))
	TahtaSayisi = Doc.Floor(Doc.Div(pi * YanakCapi, TahtaEni_cm / 100))

	TahtaHacmi = TahtaKalinligi_cm / 100 * MakaraDisGenislik * TahtaEni_cm / 100 * TahtaSayisi * KapamaYuzdesi / 100
	KapamaTahtasiInfo(0) = TahtaHacmi
	KapamaTahtasiInfo(1) = TahtaKalinligi_cm & " cm x " & TahtaEni_cm & " cm x " & MakaraDisGenislik * 100 & " cm"
	GetKapamaTahtasiInfo = KapamaTahtasiInfo
End Function

	Call RezervasyonlariHesapla()

Sub RezervasyonlariHesapla()
	QStr = "SELECT ALLOC_SIPARTNO, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, "
	QStr = QStr & " Sum(CASE WHEN ALLOC_SYSSTATUS<>'3' THEN SF_MIKTAR ELSE 0 END) SV_HARIC"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01 WHERE ALLOC_SIPNO='" & STOK40E.EVRAKNO & "'"
'	QStr = QStr & " AND ALLOC_SYSSTATUS<>'3' AND ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE<>'SP'"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY ALLOC_SIPARTNO"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgsdfhs", QStr)
	For i = 1 to STOK40T.GetRowCount()
		Set S01_1 = Doc.Select2("sdfgsdhfs", S01.GetAppName, "ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & STOK40T.ARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S01_1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
'			STOK40T.RZ_AKTIF(i) = S01_1.SF_MIKTAR(1)
			STOK40T.REZERVE_MIK(i) = S01_1.SF_MIKTAR(1)
			STOK40T.REZERVE_ED_MIKTAR(i) = STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(i) - STOK40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i) - S01_1.SV_HARIC(1) - STOK40T.S40T3_TOPLAM - STOK40T.S40T2_TOPLAM
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE", "CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MUSTERIADI", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	
	Dim MTableT1
	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SEVK_ADRESI","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "NETAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "BRUTAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "HACIM","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SIPARIS_NO","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	
	QStr1 = "SELECT STOK01.*, STOK00.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU MUSTERI,CARI00.AD MUSTERI_AD, STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH, STOK40E.CHSIPNO, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 0 NETAGIRLIK, 0 BRUTAGIRLIK, 0 HACIM, 'H' SELECTED, STOK40T.SF_IMIKTAR"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40E ON STOK40E.EVRAKNO=STOK40T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 ON CARI00.KOD=STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, VRI_NUM1, VRI_VR_R1, AMBCODE, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & "      FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & "      GROUP BY KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, VRI_NUM1, VRI_VR_R1, AMBCODE"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " ) S01 ON S01.KOD=STOK01.KOD AND S01.LOTNUMBER=STOK01.LOTNUMBER AND S01.SERINO=STOK01.SERINO "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & "   AND S01.VRI_NUM1=STOK01.VRI_NUM1 AND S01.VRI_VR_R1=STOK01.VRI_VR_R1 AND S01.AMBCODE=STOK01.AMBCODE"
	
	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=0"
	
	Set MTableT2=Doc.RunSQLQuery("MAKROTABLET2", QStr)
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	' Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "NETAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "BRUTAGIRLIK","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "HACIM","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "MUSTERI_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	' MTableT2.Addfield2 "REV_RTESTARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	' MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	
	MTable.AMBCODE = "MA"
End Sub
Sub BolunecekMakaraListesiHesapla()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	
	QStr1 = "SELECT STOK01.*, STOK00.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU MUSTERI,CARI00.AD MUSTERI_AD, STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH, STOK40E.CHSIPNO, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 0 NETAGIRLIK, 0 BRUTAGIRLIK, 0 HACIM, 'H' SELECTED, STOK40T.SF_IMIKTAR"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40E ON STOK40E.EVRAKNO=STOK40T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 ON CARI00.KOD=STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, VRI_NUM1, VRI_VR_R1, AMBCODE, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & "      FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & "      GROUP BY KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, VRI_NUM1, VRI_VR_R1, AMBCODE"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " ) S01 ON S01.KOD=STOK01.KOD AND S01.LOTNUMBER=STOK01.LOTNUMBER AND S01.SERINO=STOK01.SERINO "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & "   AND S01.VRI_NUM1=STOK01.VRI_NUM1 AND S01.VRI_VR_R1=STOK01.VRI_VR_R1 AND S01.AMBCODE=STOK01.AMBCODE"
	
	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE STOK01.ALLOC_SIPNO>' ' AND STOK01.LOTNUMBER>' '"
	QStr = QStr & " AND (S01.SF_MIKTAR>STOK01.SF_MIKTAR OR STOK01.SF_MIKTAR>STOK40T.SF_IMIKTAR * 1.15)"
	QStr = QStr & " AND TRIM(STOK01.VRI_NUM1) IS NULL"
	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK40T.PMIKTAR_UNIT NOT IN ('AD', 'KANGAL')"

	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.MUSTERIADI) <> "" Then
		if Doc.IsSQL Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(CARI00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.MUSTERIADI)) & "%'"
		Else
			QStr = QStr & " AND UCASE(CARI00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.MUSTERIADI)) & "%'"
		End if
	End if
	if trim(MTable.SIPNO) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SIPNO ='" & MTable.SIPNO & "'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.CHSIPNO) <> "" Then
		if Doc.IsSQL Then
			QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK40E.CHSIPNO) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.CHSIPNO)) & "%'"
		Else
			QStr = QStr & " AND UCASE(STOK40E.CHSIPNO) LIKE '%" & UCase(trim(MTable.CHSIPNO)) & "%'"
		End if
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY STOK01.KOD, STOK01.LOTNUMBER, STOK01.SERINO"
	
	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dde34r55", QStr)
	
	MTableT2.Empty()
	MTableT2.AppendTable(S01)
	
'	exit sub
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT2.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT2.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT2.KOD, "")
		MTableT2.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT2.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT2.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		MTableT2.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		MTableT2.MUSTERI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
		MTableT2.MUSTERI_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTableT2.MUSTERI, "")
		MTableT2.REV_RTESTARIH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "REV_RTESTARIH", S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i), "")
		MTableT2.CHSIPNO = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
		MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK = Doc.Round(GetNetAgirlik(MTableT2.KOD(i), MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)), 0)
		MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK= DOC.ceil(MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK)
		
		'   MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK= ROUND(MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK/5)*5
	Next
	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Brüt Aðýrlýk ve Hacim Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & MTableT2.GetRowCount()
		Call GetAmbalajAgirligi_Hacim(MTableT2, i, AmbalajAgirligi, Hacim)
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(i)
		MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK = MTableT2.NETAGIRLIK + AmbalajAgirligi
		MTableT2.HACIM = Hacim
	Next
	
	MTableT2.Sort("MUSTERI_AD")
	MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = 0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = 0
End Sub

Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT2.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT + MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM + MTableT2.HACIM
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_BRUT - MTableT2.BRUTAGIRLIK
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC_HACIM - MTableT2.HACIM
	End if
End Sub
Function GetNetAgirlik(StokKodu, Miktar)
	GetNetAgirlik = 0
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("we3fsddsf", "MAKROTABLET2", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&NETAGIRLIK>0", "", 1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Div(MT2.NETAGIRLIK * Miktar, MT2.SF_MIKTAR)
	Else
		GetNetAgirlik = Doc.RunMacro2("MAKR7S.AGIRLIK", "Calc_KG", StokKodu, Miktar)
	End if
End Function
Sub GetAmbalajAgirligi_Hacim(MTableT2, satirNo, AmbalajAgirligi, Hacim)
	AmbalajAgirligi = 0
	Hacim = 0
	makara = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1(satirNo)
	
'	MakaraTipi = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo)
	MakaraTipi = MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1(satirNo)
	if trim(MakaraTipi) <> "" and trim(MakaraTipi) <> "00" then
		Set A00 = Doc.Select1("Asd2q3dsaf", "AMBLJ00", "STOK_KODU='" & MakaraTipi & "'", "", -1)
		if A00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			AmbalajAgirligi = A00.AGIRLIK
			Hacim = A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_FLANSCAPI * A00.MKR_DISGENISLIK / 1000000000
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ambalaj Tanýmlarý 'nda " & MakaraTipi & " makaraya ait ambalaj tanýmý bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	End if
	
End Sub
Sub EkraniTazele()
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("wef23fsdf", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED<>'E'", "", -1)
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT2.Empty
	
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	Alanlar = MTableT2.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MT2.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			AlanDegeri = MT2.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT2.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
		Next
	Next
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub FiseEkle()
	Set STOK25E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set STOK25T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
	Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	
	if trim(STOK25E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then
		Doc.New_Voucher
		STOK25E.AMBCODE = MAKROTABLE.AMBCODE
		STOK25E.TARGETAMBCODE = MAKROTABLE.AMBCODE
		STOK25T.Empty
	End if
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.Select2("asdfsadfhsa", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK25T.AddRow
		STOK25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		
		STOK25T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
		STOK25T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK25T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
		'  STOK25T.AMBCODE_TR = MTableT.AMBCODE(i)
		'  STOK25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = MTableT.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK25T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK25T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK25T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK25T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK25T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
		STOK25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_USTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		STOK25T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK25T", "SF_MIKTAR", -1
		
		Doc.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA"
	Next
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK
End Sub
