Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : IZOLE
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ýzole Ýþ Emirleri
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set ML = Doc.Select2("sdfsehsj", "MMPS10T_LISTESI", "SELECTED10='1'", "", -1)
	satirNo = 0
	For i = 1 to ML.GetRowCount()
'if i > 10 Then Exit For

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & ML.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then satirNo = satirNo + 1
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,1).Value = "MPS No"
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,2).Value = "Stok Kodu"
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,3).Value = "Stok Adý"
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,4).Value = "Miktar"
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,5).Value = "Revize Teslim Tarihi"
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,6).Value = "Tamamlanan Miktar"
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo,7).Value = "Bakiye Miktar"

		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 1).Value = ML.EVRAKNO(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 2).Value = ML.R_MAMULKODU(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 3).Value = ML.R_MAMULKODU_STOK00_AD(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 4).Value = Doc.Round(ML.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(i),0)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 5).Value = ML.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 6).Value = Doc.Round(ML.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR(i),0)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 7).Value = Doc.Round(ML.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(i),0)

		Sh1.Range(Sh1.Rows(satirNo - 1), Sh1.Rows(satirNo)).Select
		Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

		Call HammaddeleriYaz(i, Excel, Sh1, ML, satirNo)
		Call MuhendislikTanimiYaz(i, Excel, Sh1, ML, satirNo)

		QStr = "SELECT M.*, T.MUSTERIKODU, C.AD MUSTERI, T.SF_MIKTAR SIP_MIKTARI, T.SF_IMIKTAR, TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR MAMUL_MPS_MIKTARI,"
		QStr = QStr & " S.KOD MAMUL_KODU, S.AD MAMUL_AD, S.GK_1, S.GK_3, G3.AD GK_3_AD, S.GK_4, G4.AD GK_4_AD,"
		QStr = QStr & " S.B_B_TECH_3"
		QStr = QStr & " FROM TABLE (BAGLANTI.GetKaynakMPSList('" & ML.EVRAKNO(i) & "', '')) M"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T T ON T.ARTNO=M.KAYNAK_SIPARTNO"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM TM ON TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO=T.ARTNO"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON T.MUSTERIKODU=C.KOD"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=M.MAMULSTOKKODU"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN GECOUST G3 ON G3.EVRAKNO='STKGK3' AND G3.KOD=S.GK_3"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN GECOUST G4 ON G4.EVRAKNO='STKGK4' AND G4.KOD=S.GK_4"
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE TRIM(KAYNAK_SIPNO) IS NOT NULL"

		Set KAYNAK_LIST = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdhraj", QStr)
		ToplamDamarBoyu = 0
		MaxDamarNo = 0
		Numarali = False
		For j = 1 to KAYNAK_LIST.GetRowCount()
			if j = 1 Then
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
				BaslikSatir = satirNo
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,1).Value = "Sipariþ No"
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,2).Value = "Mamul Kodu"
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,3).Value = "Mamul Adý"
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,4).Value = "Sipariþ Miktarý"
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,5).Value = "Sevk Boyu"
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,6).Value = "Müþteri"
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,7).Value = "Per/Triple/Quad"
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,8).Value = "Per/Triple/Quad Sayýsý"

				Sh1.Rows(satirNo).Select
				Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
			End if
			satirNo = satirNo + 1
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,1).Value = KAYNAK_LIST.KAYNAK_SIPNO(j)
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,2).Value = trim(KAYNAK_LIST.MAMUL_KODU(j))
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,3).Value = trim(KAYNAK_LIST.MAMUL_AD(j))
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,4).Value = KAYNAK_LIST.SIP_MIKTARI(j)
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,5).Value = KAYNAK_LIST.SF_IMIKTAR(j)
			Sh1.Cells(satirNo,6).Value = Left(KAYNAK_LIST.MUSTERI(j),20)
			if KAYNAK_LIST.B_B_TECH_3(j) <> 0 Then
				Numarali = True
				BukumElemani = Left(Right(trim(KAYNAK_LIST.MAMUL_KODU(j)),2),1)
				Gk1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_1", KAYNAK_LIST.MAMUL_KODU(j), "")
				if BukumElemani = "2" Then
					BukumElemaniAdi = "P"
					DamarSayisi = 2
				Elseif BukumElemani = "3" Then
					BukumElemaniAdi = "T"
					DamarSayisi = 3
				Elseif BukumElemani = "4" Then
					BukumElemaniAdi = "Q"
					DamarSayisi = 4
				Elseif trim(Gk1) = "MCU" Then
					BukumElemaniAdi = "M"
					DamarSayisi = 1
				Else
					BukumElemaniAdi = ""
					DamarSayisi = 0
				End if
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,7).Value = BukumElemaniAdi
				PerSayisi = Doc.Cdbl(trim(KAYNAK_LIST.GK_4(j)))
				Sh1.Cells(satirNo,8).Value = PerSayisi
				if KAYNAK_LIST.B_B_TECH_3(j) = 1 Then
					DamarNo = PerSayisi * DamarSayisi
				Else
					DamarNo = PerSayisi
				End if
				if DamarNo > 0 Then
					Sh1.Cells(satirNo,9).Value = DamarNo
				End if
				if DamarNo > MaxDamarNo Then MaxDamarNo = DamarNo
'				ToplamDamarBoyu = ToplamDamarBoyu + DamarNo * KAYNAK_LIST.SIP_MIKTARI(j)
				ToplamDamarBoyu = ToplamDamarBoyu + DamarNo * KAYNAK_LIST.MAMUL_MPS_MIKTARI(j)

				if j = KAYNAK_LIST.GetRowCount() and j > 1 Then
					Sh1.Cells(satirNo + 1,8).Value = "TOPLAM"
				End if
			End if
		Next

		if MaxDamarNo > 0 and Numarali Then
			SutunBasla = 9
			For k = 1 to MaxDamarNo
				Sh1.Cells(BaslikSatir, 9 + k - 1).Value = k
			Next
			satirNo = BaslikSatir
			For j = 1 to KAYNAK_LIST.GetRowCount()
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
'				DamarBoyu = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(ML.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(i), ToplamDamarBoyu) * KAYNAK_LIST.SIP_MIKTARI(j),0)
				DamarBoyu = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(ML.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(i), ToplamDamarBoyu) * KAYNAK_LIST.MAMUL_MPS_MIKTARI(j),0)

				DamarNo = Doc.Cdbl(Sh1.Cells(satirNo,9).Value)
				For k = 1 to DamarNo
					Sh1.Cells(SatirNo, 9 + k - 1).Value = DamarBoyu
				Next
				if j = KAYNAK_LIST.GetRowCount() and j > 1 Then
					For k = 1 to MaxDamarNo
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo + 1,9 + k - 1).Formula = "=SUM(R[-" & KAYNAK_LIST.GetRowCount() & "]C:R[-1]C)"
					Next
				End if
			Next
		End if
	Next

	Sh1.Range(Sh1.Columns(1), Sh1.Columns(100)).Select
	Excel.Selection.EntireColumn.AutoFit

	Sh1.Columns(4).Select
	Excel.Selection.Style = "Comma"
	Excel.Selection.NumberFormat = "_-* #,##0.0 _T_L_-;-* #,##0.0 _T_L_-;_-* ""-""?? _T_L_-;_-@_-"

	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Select

	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub

Sub MuhendislikTanimiYaz(OpSatirNo, Excel, Sh1, ML, satirNo)
	MamulKodu = ML.R_MAMULKODU(OpSatirNo)
	YariMamulKodu = ML.R_YMAMULKODU(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = ML.R_KAYNAKTYPE(OpSatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = ML.R_KAYNAKKODU(OpSatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = ML.R_OPERASYON(OpSatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MUSTERIKODU", ML.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo), "")
	MPSNo = ML.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo)

	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")

	if Tech.GetRecCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

'	res = pe1.Write(MuhSatirBasla, solMargin + 1, 5, Gen1, "Parametre")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, "Deðer")

	satirNo = satirNo + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 2).Value = "Üretim Parametresi"
	Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 3).Value = "Deðer"
	Sh1.Rows(satirNo).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	For i = 1 To Tech.GetRecCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 2).Value = trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("TECHSC", "AD", Tech.TSCODE(i), -1))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 3).Value = trim(Tech.TSTEXT(i))
	Next
End Sub
Sub HammaddeleriYaz(OpSatirNo, Excel, Sh1, ML, satirNo)
	QStr = "SELECT T.R_KAYNAKKODU, S.AD, T.R_MIKTAR0, S.IUNIT"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE T.EVRAKNO='" & ML.EVRAKNO(OpSatirNo) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H' AND T.R_SIRANO='" & ML.R_SIRANO(OpSatirNo) & "'"
	Set M10TH = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsefhs", QStr)
	if M10TH.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	satirNo = satirNo + 1
	Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 2).Value = "Hammadde Kodu"
	Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 3).Value = "Hammadde Adý"
	Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 4).Value = "Kullaným Miktarý"
	Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 5).Value = "Birim"
	Sh1.Rows(satirNo).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True

	For i = 1 to M10TH.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 2).Value = trim(M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(i))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 3).Value = trim(M10TH.AD(i))
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 4).Value = M10TH.R_MIKTAR0(i)
		Sh1.Cells(satirNo, 5).Value = M10TH.IUNIT(i)
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : UNT_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : DEPO
Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : MMPS10, STOK46, STOK29, STOK60, STOK30, STOK66
Açýklama : Stok Kodu Bul
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD3","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD4","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "REZERVASYON","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	QStr1 = "SELECT STOK01.*, STOK00.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, STOK00.NAME2 KOD_STOK00_NAME2,STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU, C.AD MUSTERI,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR NET_AGIRLIK, STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR + COALESCE(A.AGIRLIK,0) BRUT_AGIRLIK"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN AMBLJ00 A ON A.KOD=STOK01.VRI_VR_R1"

	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=0"
	Dim MTable2
	Set MTable2=Doc.RunSQLQuery("STOK01_LIST", QStr)

	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub Suz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_LIST")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty

	QStr1 = "SELECT STOK01.*, STOK00.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, STOK00.NAME2 KOD_STOK00_NAME2,STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU, C.AD MUSTERI,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR NET_AGIRLIK, STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR + COALESCE(A.AGIRLIK,0) BRUT_AGIRLIK"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN AMBLJ00 A ON A.KOD=STOK01.VRI_VR_R1"

	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=1"
	if trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD1),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD2),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD3),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD4),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK01.AMBCODE) = '" & UCase(trim(MTable.AMBCODE)) & "'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.MUSTERI) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(C.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(rtrim(MTable.MUSTERI)) & "%'"
	End if
	if MTable.REZERVASYON = "0" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	Elseif MTable.REZERVASYON = "1" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS<>' '"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.SIPNO) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SIPNO='" &  MTable.SIPNO & "'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY STOK01.KOD, STOK01.LOTNUMBER, STOK01.SERINO"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("STOK01XXXX_1", QStr)
	MTable2.Empty
	MTable2.AppendTable(S01)
	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub Sec()
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	StokKodu = MTable2.KOD
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	S40T.KOD = StokKodu
End Sub
Sub Sec()
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	satirno =  S40T.GetRowCount()
	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdfgsdfhsj", "MAKROTABLE2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to MT.GetRowCount() 			
		S40T.SetCurrentRow(satirno)
		S40T.KOD(satirno) = MT.KOD(i)
		S40T.RTESTARIH	(satirno) = MT.T_TARIH(i)
		satirno = S40T.AddRow()	
	Next 
	MTable2.empty
	S40T.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set B01E = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
	Set B01T = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
	B01T.SafePack()

	if LISTET.GetRowCount() > 0 then
		For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
			Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		Next
		Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
End Sub

Public MTable2
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD1","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD2","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD3","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AD4","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "REZERVASYON","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	QStr1 = "SELECT STOK01.*, STOK00.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, STOK00.NAME2 KOD_STOK00_NAME2,STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU, C.AD MUSTERI,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR NET_AGIRLIK, STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR + COALESCE(A.AGIRLIK,0) BRUT_AGIRLIK"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN AMBLJ00 A ON A.KOD=STOK01.VRI_VR_R1"

	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=0"
	Dim MTable2
	Set MTable2=Doc.RunSQLQuery("STOK01_LIST", QStr)
	MTable2.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable2.Addfield2 "CU_AGR","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	MTable.REZERVASYON = "2"
	MTable.AMBCODE = "MA"

	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub Suz()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_LIST")
	MTable2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTable2.Empty

	bos = " "
	QStr1 = "SELECT STOK01.*, STOK00.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, STOK00.NAME2 KOD_STOK00_NAME2,STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU, C.AD MUSTERI,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR NET_AGIRLIK, STOK00.B_AGIRLIK * STOK01.SF_MIKTAR + COALESCE(A.AGIRLIK,0) BRUT_AGIRLIK"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T ON STOK40T.ARTNO=STOK01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN AMBLJ00 A ON A.KOD=STOK01.VRI_VR_R1"

	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=1"
	if trim(MTable.AD1) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD1),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD2) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD2),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD3) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD3),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AD4) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK00.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(replace(rTrim(MTable.AD4),"_"," ")) & "%'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(STOK01.AMBCODE) = '" & UCase(trim(MTable.AMBCODE)) & "'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.MUSTERI) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND UPPER(C.AD) LIKE '%" & UCase(rtrim(MTable.MUSTERI)) & "%'"
	End if

	if MTable.REZERVASYON = "0" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='"& bos &"'"
	Elseif MTable.REZERVASYON = "1" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS<>'"& bos &"'"
	End if

	if trim(MTable.SIPNO) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.ALLOC_SIPNO='" &  MTable.SIPNO & "'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY STOK01.KOD, STOK01.LOTNUMBER, STOK01.SERINO"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("STOK01XXXX_1", QStr)
	MTable2.Empty
	MTable2.AppendTable(S01)

	MTable2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
